7. Look at `desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_dimensions.py` for `scorecard_subjective_entries` — how does it decide what's subjective?

Be very thorough. I want to understand whether the concept of "is this dimension subjective?" has a canonical home, or if it's being re-derived ad-hoc in multiple places.

   100→
   101→def _build_dimensions() -> list[Dimension]:
   102→    """Derive dimensions from DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.
   103→
   104→    Each unique (dimension, tier) pair becomes a Dimension, with its detectors
   105→    collected automatically. Order follows first-seen in the policies dict.
   106→    """
   107→    # Collect (dimension_name -> tier) preserving first-seen order,
   108→    # and group detectors by dimension.
   109→    dim_tiers: dict[str, int] = {}
   110→    grouped: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   111→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items():
   112→        if policy.dimension is None or policy.tier is None:
   113→            continue
   114→        if policy.dimension not in dim_tiers:
   115→            dim_tiers[policy.dimension] = policy.tier
   116→            grouped[policy.dimension] = []
   117→        grouped[policy.dimension].append(detector)
   118→    return [
   119→        Dimension(name=name, tier=tier, detectors=grouped[name])
   120→        for name, tier in dim_tiers.items()
   121→    ]
   122→
   123→
   124→DIMENSIONS = _build_dimensions()
   125→DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME = {d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS}
   126→
   127→TIER_WEIGHTS = {
   128→    Tier.AUTO_FIX: 1,
   129→    Tier.QUICK_FIX: 2,
   130→    Tier.JUDGMENT: 3,
   131→    Tier.MAJOR_REFACTOR: 4,
   132→}
   133→CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS = {Confidence.HIGH: 1.0, Confidence.MEDIUM: 0.7, Confidence.LOW: 0.3}
   134→
   135→# Minimum checks for full dimension weight — below this, weight is dampened
   136→# proportionally. Prevents small-sample dimensions from swinging the overall score.
   137→MIN_SAMPLE = 200
   138→
   139→# Holistic review weight: findings with file="." and detail.holistic=True
   140→# get a 10x weight multiplier for display/priority purposes (issues list,
   141→# remediation engine).  NOT used in score computation — review findings are
   142→# excluded from the detection scoring pipeline (scored via assessments only).
   143→HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER = 10.0
   144→HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL = 10
   145→
   146→# Budget: subjective dimensions get this fraction of the overall score.
   147→# Mechanical dimensions get the remainder.
   148→SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.60
   149→MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 1.0 - SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   150→
   151→# Per-dimension weighting within the mechanical pool.
   152→# Keep this balanced: no special boost for security/test in the pool itself.
   153→MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   154→    "file health": 2.0,
   155→    "code quality": 1.0,
   156→    "duplication": 1.0,
   157→    "test health": 1.0,
   158→    "security": 1.0,
   159→}
   160→
   161→# Per-dimension weighting within the subjective pool.
   162→# Emphasize elegance and contract/type coherence for a stronger architecture north star.
   163→SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   164→    "high elegance": 22.0,
   165→    "mid elegance": 22.0,
   166→    "low elegance": 12.0,
   167→    "contracts": 12.0,
   168→    "type safety": 12.0,
   169→    "abstraction fit": 8.0,
   170→    "logic clarity": 6.0,
   171→    # Low-but-meaningful structural signal (about half of the subjective
   172→    # average weight) so it matters without dominating craftsmanship axes.
   173→    "structure nav": 5.0,
   174→    "error consistency": 3.0,
   175→    "naming quality": 2.0,
   176→    "ai generated debt": 1.0,
   177→    "design coherence": 10.0,
   178→}
   179→
   180→# Synthetic check count for subjective dimensions in dimension_scores.
   181→SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS = 10
   182→
   183→FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE: dict[ScoreMode, frozenset[str]] = {
   184→    "lenient": frozenset({"open"}),
   185→    "strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix"}),
   186→    "verified_strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "fixed", "false_positive"}),
   187→}
   188→
   189→# Tolerance for treating a subjective score as "on target" in integrity checks.
   190→# Scores within this band of the target are flagged as potential gaming.
   191→SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE = 0.05
   192→
   193→
   194→def matches_target_score(
   195→    score: object,
   196→    target: object,
   197→    *,
   198→    tolerance: float = SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
   199→) -> bool:
   200→    """Return True when score is within tolerance of target."""
   201→    try:
   202→        score_value = float(score)
   203→        target_value = float(target)
   204→        tolerance_value = max(0.0, float(tolerance))
   205→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   206→        return False
   207→    return abs(score_value - target_value) <= tolerance_value
   208→
   209→
   210→def register_scoring_policy(policy: DetectorScoringPolicy) -> None:
   211→    """Register a scoring policy at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   212→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES[policy.detector] = policy
   213→    _rebuild_derived()
   214→
   215→
   216→def _rebuild_derived() -> None:
   217→    """Rebuild DIMENSIONS, DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME, FILE_BASED_DETECTORS from current state.
   218→
   219→    Mutates existing objects in-place so that all references (including imports
   220→    that bound the original objects) see the updates.
   221→    """
   222→    new_dims = _build_dimensions()
   223→    DIMENSIONS.clear()
   224→    DIMENSIONS.extend(new_dims)
   225→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.clear()
   226→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.update({d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS})
   227→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.clear()
   228→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.update(
   229→        det for det, pol in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items() if pol.file_based
   230→    )
   231→
   232→
   233→def detector_policy(detector: str) -> DetectorScoringPolicy:
   234→    """Get scoring policy for a detector, with a safe default fallback."""
   235→    return DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(
   236→        detector,
   237→        DetectorScoringPolicy(detector=detector, dimension=None, tier=None),
   238→    )
   239→
   240→

   242→    "CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS",

    21→    "package_organization": "Structure nav",
    22→    "high_level_elegance": "High elegance",
    23→    "mid_level_elegance": "Mid elegance",
    24→    "low_level_elegance": "Low elegance",
    25→    # Design coherence (concerns bridge)
    26→    "design_coherence": "Design coherence",
    27→    # Per-file review dimensions
    28→    "naming_quality": "Naming quality",
    29→    "logic_clarity": "Logic clarity",
    30→    "type_safety": "Type safety",
    31→    "contract_coherence": "Contracts",
    32→}
    33→
    34→def _display_fallback(dim_name: str) -> str:
    35→    words = dim_name.replace("_", " ")
    36→    return words[0].upper() + words[1:] if words else words
    37→
    38→
    39→def _normalize_dimension_key(dim_name: object) -> str:
    40→    if not isinstance(dim_name, str):
    41→        return ""
    42→    return "_".join(dim_name.strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())
    43→
    44→
    45→def _primary_lang_from_findings(findings: dict) -> str | None:
    46→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    47→    for finding in findings.values():
    48→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
    49→            continue
    50→        raw_lang = finding.get("lang")
    51→        if not isinstance(raw_lang, str) or not raw_lang.strip():
    52→            continue
    53→        key = raw_lang.strip().lower()
    54→        counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    55→    if not counts:
    56→        return None
    57→    return max(counts, key=counts.get)
    58→
    59→
    60→def _dimension_display_name(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> str:
    61→    try:
    62→        # Deferred import to avoid circular: scoring -> _scoring/results/core
    63→        # -> _scoring/subjective/core -> intelligence.review -> state -> scoring
    64→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    65→            dimension_display_name,
    66→        )
    67→
    68→        return str(dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    69→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    70→        return DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim_name, _display_fallback(dim_name))
    71→
    72→
    73→def _dimension_weight(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> float:
    74→    try:
    75→        # Deferred import to avoid circular (see _dimension_display_name).
    76→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    77→            dimension_weight,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→        return float(dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    81→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    82→        return 1.0
    83→
    84→
    85→def append_subjective_dimensions(
    86→    results: dict,
    87→    findings: dict,
    88→    assessments: dict | None,
    89→    failure_set: frozenset[str],
    90→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    91→) -> None:
    92→    """Append subjective review dimensions to results dict (mutates results).
    93→
    94→    Subjective scoring is evidence-first: open review findings for a dimension
    95→    determine pass-rate, while imported assessment scores are retained as
    96→    metadata for transparency.
    97→    """
    98→    # Deferred import to avoid circular (see _dimension_display_name).
    99→    from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.holistic import DIMENSIONS
   100→
   101→    raw_defaults = DIMENSIONS
   102→    allowed = (
   103→        {_normalize_dimension_key(name) for name in allowed_dimensions}
   104→        if allowed_dimensions is not None
   105→        else None
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    default_dimensions = []
   109→    for raw_dim in raw_defaults:
   110→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
   111→        if not dim:
   112→            continue
   113→        if allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
   114→            continue
   115→        default_dimensions.append(dim)
   116→
   117→    assessed: dict[str, dict] = {}
   118→    for raw_dim, payload in (assessments or {}).items():
   119→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
   120→        if not dim:
   121→            continue
   122→        # Real assessments (from review imports) always count regardless of
   123→        # allowed_dimensions — they were deliberately imported and should not
   124→        # be silently discarded.  However, system-generated placeholders
   125→        # (scan_reset_subjective) are not real assessments and should respect
   126→        # the allowed_dimensions scope like defaults.
   127→        is_placeholder = isinstance(payload, dict) and (
   128→            payload.get("placeholder") is True
   129→            or payload.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   130→            or payload.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   131→        )
   132→        if is_placeholder and allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
   133→            continue
   134→        assessed[dim] = payload
   135→    existing_lower = {k.lower() for k in results}
   136→    lang_name = _primary_lang_from_findings(findings)
   137→
   138→    all_dims = list(default_dimensions)
   139→    for dim_name in assessed:
   140→        if dim_name not in default_dimensions:
   141→            all_dims.append(dim_name)
   142→
   143→    for dim_name in all_dims:
   144→        is_default = dim_name in default_dimensions
   145→        assessment = assessed.get(dim_name)
   146→        has_assessment = isinstance(assessment, dict)
   147→        if not is_default and not assessment:
   148→            continue
   149→
   150→        display = _dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   151→        if display.lower() in existing_lower:
   152→            display = f"{display} (subjective)"
   153→
   154→        # Count open review/concern findings for display (work queue), but
   155→        # these do NOT drive the dimension score — only assessment scores do.
   156→        issue_count = sum(
   157→            1
   158→            for finding in findings.values()
   159→            if finding.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   160→            and finding.get("status") in failure_set
   161→            and _normalize_dimension_key(
   162→                finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension")
   163→            )
   164→            == dim_name
   165→        )
   166→
   167→        assessment_score = (
   168→            max(0.0, min(100.0, float(assessment.get("score", 0))))
   169→            if isinstance(assessment, dict)
   170→            else 0.0
   171→        )
   172→        integrity_penalty = (
   173→            assessment.get("integrity_penalty")
   174→            if isinstance(assessment, dict)
   175→            else None
   176→        )
   177→        reset_pending = bool(
   178→            isinstance(assessment, dict)
   179→            and (
   180→                assessment.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   181→                or assessment.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   182→                or assessment.get("placeholder") is True
   183→            )
   184→        )
   185→        if reset_pending:
   186→            score = 0.0
   187→            pass_rate = 0.0
   188→        elif integrity_penalty == "target_match_reset":
   189→            score = 0.0
   190→            pass_rate = 0.0
   191→        elif has_assessment:
   192→            # Assessment score drives the dimension score directly.
   193→            # Resolving review findings does NOT change this score —
   194→            # only a fresh review import updates it.
   195→            score = assessment_score
   196→            pass_rate = score / 100.0
   197→        else:
   198→            # No assessment yet: explicit unassessed placeholder.
   199→            # Subjective dimensions start at 0 until an assessment is imported.
   200→            score = 0.0
   201→            pass_rate = 0.0
   202→        components: list[str] = []
   203→        component_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   204→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
   205→            raw_components = assessment.get("components")
   206→            if isinstance(raw_components, list):
   207→                components = [
   208→                    str(item).strip()
   209→                    for item in raw_components
   210→                    if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
   211→                ]
   212→            raw_component_scores = assessment.get("component_scores")
   213→            if isinstance(raw_component_scores, dict):
   214→                for key, value in raw_component_scores.items():
   215→                    if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
   216→                        continue
   217→                    if not is_numeric(value):
   218→                        continue
   219→                    component_scores[key.strip()] = round(
   220→                        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))),
   221→                        1,
   222→                    )
   223→
   224→        results[display] = {
   225→            "score": round(float(score), 1),
   226→            "tier": 4,
   227→            "checks": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   228→            "issues": issue_count,
   229→            "detectors": {
   230→                "subjective_assessment": {
   231→                    "potential": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   232→                    "pass_rate": round(pass_rate, 4),
   233→                    "issues": issue_count,
   234→                    "weighted_failures": round(SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS * (1 - pass_rate), 4),
   235→                    "assessment_score": round(assessment_score, 1),
   236→                    "placeholder": reset_pending or not has_assessment,
   237→                    "dimension_key": dim_name,
   238→                    "configured_weight": round(
   239→                        _dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name), 6
   240→                    ),
   241→                    "components": components,
   242→                    **(
   243→                        {"component_scores": component_scores}
   244→                        if component_scores
   245→                        else {}
   246→                    ),
   247→                }
   248→            },
   249→        }
   250→
   251→
   252→__all__ = [
   253→    "DISPLAY_NAMES",
   254→    "append_subjective_dimensions",
   255→]
   256→
```

    51→def scorecard_dimension_entries(
    52→    state: dict,
    53→    *,

    56→    """Return scorecard rows with presentation-friendly metadata."""

    75→        not_scanned = bool(

    80→        )
    81→        dim_key = str(subjective_meta.get("dimension_key", "")) if subjective_meta else ""
    82→        stale = bool(subjective_meta.get("stale")) if subjective_meta else False

    97→                "stale": stale,

   100→                "carried_forward": carried_forward,

    31→)

    33→
    34→def _show_concerns(state: dict, lang_name: str | None) -> None:
    35→    """Render current design concerns from mechanical signals."""
    36→    from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
    37→
    38→    concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
    39→    if not concerns:
    40→        print(colorize("  No design concerns detected.", "green"))
    41→        return
    42→
    43→    print(colorize(f"\n  {len(concerns)} design concern(s):\n", "bold"))
    44→    dismissals = state.get("concern_dismissals", {})
    45→
    46→    for i, c in enumerate(concerns, 1):
    47→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. [{c.type}] {c.file}", "cyan"))
    48→        print(f"     {c.summary}")
    49→        for ev in c.evidence:
    50→            print(colorize(f"       - {ev}", "dim"))
    51→        print(colorize(f"     ? {c.question}", "yellow"))
    52→
    53→        # Check if previously dismissed (but resurface due to changed findings).
    54→        prev = dismissals.get(c.fingerprint)
    55→        if isinstance(prev, dict):
    56→            reasoning = prev.get("reasoning", "")
    57→            if reasoning:
    58→                print(colorize(f"     (previously dismissed: {reasoning})", "dim"))
    59→        print()
    60→
    61→
    62→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    63→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
    64→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    65→    state = runtime.state
    66→    config = runtime.config
    67→
    68→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    69→        return
    70→
    71→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    72→    if stale_warning:
    73→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    74→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    75→    if skill_warning:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    77→
    78→    # Handle "show concerns" as a special view.
    79→    pattern_raw = getattr(args, "pattern", "")
    80→    if pattern_raw and pattern_raw.strip().lower() == "concerns":
    81→        lang = resolve_lang(args)
    82→        _show_concerns(state, lang.name if lang else None)
    83→        return
    84→
    85→    show_code = getattr(args, "code", False)
    86→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
    87→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
    88→    if not ok or pattern is None:
    89→        return
    90→
    91→    matches = load_matches(state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic)
    92→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    93→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    94→    narrative = compute_narrative(
    95→        state,
    96→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
    97→    )
    98→
    99→    if not matches:
   100→        # Try interpreting the pattern as a dimension name/key
   101→        dim_matches, dim_display = load_matches_for_dimension(
   102→            state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter
   103→        )
   104→        if dim_matches:
   105→            matches = dim_matches
   106→            pattern = dim_display or pattern
   107→        else:
   108→            # Check if this is a subjective dimension (via DISPLAY_NAMES or dimension_scores)
   109→            lowered = pattern_raw.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_") if pattern_raw else ""
   110→            if lowered:
   111→                # 1. Check mechanical dimensions (DIMENSIONS list)
   112→                dim_lookup = {dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_"): dim for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS}
   113→                matched_dim = dim_lookup.get(lowered)

1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
   158→
   159→__all__ = [
   160→    "dimension_cli_key",
   161→    "scorecard_dimension_cli_keys",
   162→    "scorecard_dimension_rows",
   163→    "scorecard_dimensions_payload",
   164→    "scorecard_subjective_entries",
   165→]
   166→

     2→

    28→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29→class ScoreBundle:
    30→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    31→    strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    32→    verified_strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    33→    overall_score: float
    34→    objective_score: float
    35→    strict_score: float
    36→    verified_strict_score: float

    39→def compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
    40→    findings: dict,
    41→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    42→    *,
    43→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
    44→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    45→) -> dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]]:
    46→    """Compute dimension scores for lenient/strict/verified_strict in one pass."""
    47→    results: dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]] = {mode: {} for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    48→
    49→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    50→        totals = {
    51→            mode: {
    52→                "checks": 0,
    53→                "issues": 0,
    54→                "weighted_failures": 0.0,
    55→                "detectors": {},
    56→            }
    57→            for mode in SCORING_MODES
    58→        }
    59→
    60→        for detector in dim.detectors:
    61→            potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    62→            if potential <= 0:
    63→                continue
    64→
    65→            detector_stats = detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)
    66→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    67→                pass_rate, issues, weighted = detector_stats[mode]
    68→                totals[mode]["checks"] += potential
    69→                totals[mode]["issues"] += issues
    70→                totals[mode]["weighted_failures"] += weighted
    71→                totals[mode]["detectors"][detector] = {
    72→                    "potential": potential,
    73→                    "pass_rate": pass_rate,
    74→                    "issues": issues,
    75→                    "weighted_failures": weighted,
    76→                }
    77→
    78→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    79→            total_checks = totals[mode]["checks"]
    80→            if total_checks <= 0:
    81→                continue
    82→            dim_score = (
    83→                max(
    84→                    0.0,
    85→                    (total_checks - totals[mode]["weighted_failures"]) / total_checks,
    86→                )
    87→                * 100
    88→            )
    89→            results[mode][dim.name] = {
    90→                "score": round(dim_score, 1),
    91→                "tier": dim.tier,
    92→                "checks": total_checks,
    93→                "issues": totals[mode]["issues"],
    94→                "detectors": totals[mode]["detectors"],
    95→            }
    96→
    97→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    98→        append_subjective_dimensions(
    99→            results[mode],
   100→            findings,
   101→            subjective_assessments,
   102→            FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode],
   103→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   104→        )
   105→    return results
   106→
   107→
   108→def compute_dimension_scores(
   109→    findings: dict,
   110→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   111→    *,
   112→    strict: bool = False,
   113→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   114→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   115→) -> dict[str, dict]:
   116→    """Compute per-dimension scores from findings and potentials."""
   117→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   118→    return compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   119→        findings,
   120→        potentials,
   121→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   122→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   123→    )[mode]
   124→
   125→
   126→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
   127→    return " ".join(str(name).strip().lower().split())
   128→
   129→
   130→def _mechanical_dimension_weight(name: str) -> float:
   131→    return float(
   132→        MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
   133→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
   134→            1.0,
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→
   138→
   139→def _subjective_dimension_weight(name: str, data: dict) -> float:
   140→    subjective_meta = (
   141→        data.get("detectors", {}).get("subjective_assessment", {})
   142→        if isinstance(data, dict)
   143→        else {}
   144→    )
   145→    configured = (
   146→        subjective_meta.get("configured_weight")
   147→        if isinstance(subjective_meta, dict)
   148→        else None
   149→    )
   150→    if is_numeric(configured):
   151→        return max(0.0, float(configured))
   152→
   153→    return float(
   154→        SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
   155→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
   156→            1.0,
   157→        )
   158→    )
   159→
   160→
   161→def compute_health_breakdown(
   162→    dimension_scores: dict, *, score_key: str = "score"
   163→) -> dict[str, object]:
   164→    """Return pool averages and weighted contribution breakdown for score transparency."""
   165→    if not dimension_scores:
   166→        return {
   167→            "overall_score": 100.0,
   168→            "mechanical_fraction": 1.0,
   169→            "subjective_fraction": 0.0,
   170→            "mechanical_avg": 100.0,
   171→            "subjective_avg": None,
   172→            "entries": [],
   173→        }
   174→
   175→    mech_sum = 0.0
   176→    mech_weight = 0.0
   177→    subj_sum = 0.0
   178→    subj_weight = 0.0
   179→    mechanical_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   180→    subjective_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   181→
   182→    for name, data in dimension_scores.items():
   183→        score = float(data.get(score_key, data.get("score", 0.0)))
   184→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
   185→        if is_subjective:
   186→            configured = max(0.0, _subjective_dimension_weight(name, data))
   187→            effective = configured
   188→            subj_sum += score * effective
   189→            subj_weight += effective
   190→            subjective_rows.append(
   191→                {
   192→                    "name": str(name),
   193→                    "score": score,
   194→                    "configured_weight": configured,
   195→                    "effective_weight": effective,
   196→                }
   197→            )
   198→            continue
   199→
   200→        checks = float(data.get("checks", 0) or 0)
   201→        sample_factor = min(1.0, checks / MIN_SAMPLE) if checks > 0 else 0.0
   202→        configured = max(0.0, _mechanical_dimension_weight(name))
   203→        effective = configured * sample_factor
   204→        mech_sum += score * effective
   205→        mech_weight += effective
   206→        mechanical_rows.append(
   207→            {
   208→                "name": str(name),
   209→                "score": score,
   210→                "checks": checks,
   211→                "sample_factor": sample_factor,
   212→                "configured_weight": configured,
   213→                "effective_weight": effective,
   214→            }
   215→        )
   216→
   217→    mech_avg = (mech_sum / mech_weight) if mech_weight > 0 else 100.0
   218→    subj_avg = (subj_sum / subj_weight) if subj_weight > 0 else None
   219→
   220→    if subj_avg is None:
   221→        mechanical_fraction = 1.0
   222→        subjective_fraction = 0.0
   223→        overall_score = round(mech_avg, 1)
   224→    elif mech_weight == 0:
   225→        mechanical_fraction = 0.0
   226→        subjective_fraction = 1.0
   227→        overall_score = round(subj_avg, 1)
   228→    else:
   229→        mechanical_fraction = MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   230→        subjective_fraction = SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   231→        overall_score = round(
   232→            mech_avg * mechanical_fraction + subj_avg * subjective_fraction,
   233→            1,
   234→        )
   235→
   236→    entries: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   237→
   238→    for row in mechanical_rows:
   239→        pool_share = (
   240→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / mech_weight if mech_weight > 0 else 0.0
   241→        )
   242→        per_point = mechanical_fraction * pool_share
   243→        score = float(row["score"])
   244→        entries.append(
   245→            {
   246→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   247→                "pool": "mechanical",
   248→                "score": score,
   249→                "checks": float(row["checks"]),
   250→                "sample_factor": float(row["sample_factor"]),
```

    34→def _show_concerns(state: dict, lang_name: str | None) -> None:
    35→    """Render current design concerns from mechanical signals."""
    36→    from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
    37→
    38→    concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
    39→    if not concerns:
    40→        print(colorize("  No design concerns detected.", "green"))
    41→        return
    42→
    43→    print(colorize(f"\n  {len(concerns)} design concern(s):\n", "bold"))
    44→    dismissals = state.get("concern_dismissals", {})
    45→
    46→    for i, c in enumerate(concerns, 1):
    47→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. [{c.type}] {c.file}", "cyan"))
    48→        print(f"     {c.summary}")
    49→        for ev in c.evidence:
    50→            print(colorize(f"       - {ev}", "dim"))
    51→        print(colorize(f"     ? {c.question}", "yellow"))
    52→
    53→        # Check if previously dismissed (but resurface due to changed findings).

    56→            reasoning = prev.get("reasoning", "")
    57→            if reasoning:
    58→                print(colorize(f"     (previously dismissed: {reasoning})", "dim"))
    59→        print()

   251→        show_code=show_code,
   252→        top=top,
   253→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   254→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   255→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   256→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   257→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   258→    )
   259→    try:
   260→        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan as _load_plan
   261→        _plan = _load_plan()
   262→        _plan_active = _plan if (_plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")) else None
   263→    except Exception:
   264→        _plan_active = None
   265→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches, plan=_plan_active)
   266→    show_subjective_followup(
   267→        state,
   268→        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   269→    )
   270→
   271→    # Phase 5: naming guide for subjective views
   272→    if pattern_raw and pattern_raw.strip().lower() in ("subjective", "subjective_review"):
   273→        print(colorize("  Related views:", "dim"))
   274→        print(colorize("    `show review --status open`            Per-file design review findings", "dim"))
   275→        print(colorize("    `show subjective_review --status open`  Files needing re-review", "dim"))
   276→
   277→
   278→__all__ = ["cmd_show"]
   279→

1→"""Scope and queue helpers for show command selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     6→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     7→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     8→    QueueBuildOptions,
     9→    build_work_queue,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    12→
    13→
    14→def _build_dimension_lookup() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    15→    """Build a map from dimension name/key (lowered) to detector names."""
    16→    lookup: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    17→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    18→        detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
    19→        lookup[dim.name.lower()] = detectors
    20→        # Also index by underscore key: "file_health" -> detectors
    21→        key = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    22→        if key not in lookup:
    23→            lookup[key] = detectors
    24→    # Also add DISPLAY_NAMES reverse lookup (e.g. "abstraction_fit" -> "Abstraction Fit")
    25→    for key, display in scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        normalized_key = key.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    27→        normalized_display = display.lower()
    28→        # Find which dimension this belongs to via get_dimension_for_detector or direct name match
    29→        for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    30→            dim_lower = dim.name.lower()
    31→            if normalized_display == dim_lower or normalized_key == dim_lower.replace(" ", "_"):
    32→                detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
    33→                if normalized_key not in lookup:
    34→                    lookup[normalized_key] = detectors
    35→                if normalized_display not in lookup:
    36→                    lookup[normalized_display] = detectors
    37→    return lookup
    38→
    39→
    40→def try_dimension_rewrite(pattern: str) -> str | None:
    41→    """If pattern matches a dimension name/key, return a detector scope. Otherwise None."""
    42→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    43→    lowered = pattern.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    44→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered)
    45→    if not detectors:
    46→        # Try display name form (with spaces)
    47→        detectors = lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    48→    if not detectors:
    49→        return None
    50→    # Return first detector as scope — the work queue will match on detector name.
    51→    # For multi-detector dimensions, we can't pass multiple scopes through the current
    52→    # interface, but most dimensions have one primary detector. Use glob pattern.
    53→    if len(detectors) == 1:
    54→        return detectors[0]
    55→    # For multi-detector dimensions, use wildcard pattern that scope_matches handles
    56→    return None  # Fall through to load_matches_for_dimension
    57→
    58→
    59→def load_matches_for_dimension(
    60→    state: dict,
    61→    pattern: str,
    62→    *,
    63→    status_filter: str,
    64→) -> tuple[list[dict], str | None]:
    65→    """Try to load findings matching a dimension name. Returns (matches, rewritten_pattern)."""
    66→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    67→    lowered = pattern.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    68→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered) or lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    69→    if not detectors:
    70→        return [], None
    71→
    72→    # Load findings for each detector in this dimension
    73→    all_matches: list[dict] = []
    74→    for detector in detectors:
    75→        matches = load_matches(
    76→            state, scope=detector, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=False
    77→        )
    78→        all_matches.extend(matches)
    79→    # Deduplicate by finding ID
    80→    seen: set[str] = set()
    81→    unique: list[dict] = []
    82→    for item in all_matches:
    83→        fid = item.get("id", "")
    84→        if fid not in seen:
    85→            seen.add(fid)
    86→            unique.append(item)
    87→
    88→    # Find the display name for the dimension
    89→    dim_display = pattern
    90→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    91→        if dim.name.lower() == lowered or dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
    92→            dim_display = dim.name
    93→            break
    94→
    95→    return unique, dim_display
    96→
    97→
    98→def _detector_names_hint() -> str:
    99→    """Return a compact list of detector names for the help message."""
   100→    from desloppify.core import registry as registry_mod
   101→    names = getattr(registry_mod, "DISPLAY_ORDER", [])
   102→    if names:
   103→        return ", ".join(names[:10]) + (", ..." if len(names) > 10 else "")
   104→    return "smells, structural, security, review, ..."
   105→
   106→
   107→def resolve_show_scope(args) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str, str | None]:
   108→    """Resolve scope/pattern/status for a show invocation."""
   109→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   110→    pattern = args.pattern
   111→    status_filter = "open" if chronic else getattr(args, "status", "open")
   112→    if chronic:
   113→        scope = pattern
   114→        pattern = pattern or "<chronic>"
   115→        return True, pattern, status_filter, scope
   116→    if not pattern:
   117→        print(
   118→            colorize(
   119→                "Pattern required (or use --chronic). Try: desloppify show --help",
   120→                "yellow",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→        return False, None, status_filter, ""
   124→    return True, pattern, status_filter, pattern
   125→
   126→
   127→def load_matches(
   128→    state: dict,
   129→    *,
   130→    scope: str | None,
   131→    status_filter: str,
   132→    chronic: bool,
   133→) -> list[dict]:
   134→    """Load matching findings from the ranked queue."""
   135→    queue = build_work_queue(
   136→        state,
   137→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   138→            count=None,
   139→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   140→            scope=scope,
   141→            status=status_filter,
   142→            include_subjective=False,
   143→            chronic=chronic,
   144→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   145→        ),
   146→    )
   147→    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
   148→
   149→
   150→def resolve_noise(config: dict, matches: list[dict]):
   151→    """Apply detector/global noise budget to show matches."""
   152→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   153→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   154→    )
   155→    surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   156→        matches,
   157→        budget=noise_budget,
   158→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   159→    )
   160→    return (
   161→        surfaced_matches,
   162→        hidden_by_detector,
   163→        noise_budget,
   164→        global_noise_budget,
   165→        budget_warning,
   166→    )
   167→
   168→
   169→__all__ = [
   170→    "_detector_names_hint",
   171→    "load_matches",
   172→    "load_matches_for_dimension",
   173→    "resolve_noise",
   174→    "resolve_show_scope",
   175→]
   176→

1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     9→    ALL_STATUSES,
    10→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    11→    build_subjective_items,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    15→    build_finding_items,
    16→    choose_fallback_tier,
    17→    group_queue_items,
    18→    item_explain,
    19→    item_sort_key,
    20→    tier_counts,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class QueueBuildOptions:
    27→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    28→
    29→    tier: int | None = None
    30→    count: int | None = 1
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    scope: str | None = None
    33→    status: str = "open"
    34→    include_subjective: bool = True
    35→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    36→    chronic: bool = False
    37→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    38→    explain: bool = False
    39→    plan: dict | None = None
    40→    include_deferred: bool = False
    41→    cluster: str | None = None
    42→
    43→
    44→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    45→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    46→
    47→    items: list[dict]
    48→    total: int
    49→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    50→    requested_tier: int | None
    51→    selected_tier: int | None
    52→    fallback_reason: str | None
    53→    available_tiers: list[int]
    54→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    55→
    56→
    57→def _apply_plan_order(
    58→    items: list[dict],
    59→    plan: dict,
    60→    *,
    61→    include_deferred: bool = False,
    62→    cluster: str | None = None,
    63→) -> list[dict]:
    64→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    65→
    66→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    67→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    68→    3. Deferred items are appended last (or excluded).
    69→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    70→    """
    71→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    72→    deferred_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("deferred", []))
    73→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    74→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    75→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    76→
    77→    # Build lookup
    78→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    79→    for item in items:
    80→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    81→
    82→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    83→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    84→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    85→        if override.get("description"):
    86→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    87→        if override.get("note"):
    88→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    89→        if override.get("cluster"):
    90→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    91→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    92→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    93→                "name": cluster_name,
    94→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    95→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    96→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    97→            }
    98→
    99→    # Split into ordered, remaining, deferred
   100→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   101→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   102→    for fid in queue_order:
   103→        if fid in by_id and fid not in deferred_ids:
   104→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   105→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   106→
   107→    deferred_items: list[dict] = []
   108→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   109→    for item in items:
   110→        item_id = item["id"]
   111→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   112→            continue
   113→        if item_id in deferred_ids:
   114→            deferred_items.append(item)
   115→        else:
   116→            remaining.append(item)
   117→
   118→    # Assign queue positions
   119→    result = ordered + remaining
   120→    if include_deferred:
   121→        result = result + deferred_items
   122→
   123→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   124→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   125→        if item["id"] in deferred_ids:
   126→            item["plan_deferred"] = True
   127→
   128→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   129→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   130→    if effective_cluster:
   131→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   132→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   133→        if cluster_member_ids:
   134→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   135→
   136→    return result
   137→
   138→
   139→def build_work_queue(
   140→    state: StateModel,
   141→    *,
   142→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   143→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   144→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   145→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   146→
   147→    status = resolved_options.status
   148→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   149→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   150→    try:
   151→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   152→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   153→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   154→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   155→
   156→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   157→        state,
   158→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   159→        status_filter=status,
   160→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   161→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   162→    )
   163→
   164→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   165→    if (
   166→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   167→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   168→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   169→    ):
   170→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   171→            state,
   172→            state.get("findings", {}),
   173→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   174→        )
   175→        for item in subjective_items:
   176→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   177→                all_items.append(item)
   178→
   179→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   180→
   181→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   182→    if resolved_options.plan:
   183→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   184→            all_items,
   185→            resolved_options.plan,
   186→            include_deferred=resolved_options.include_deferred,
   187→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   188→        )
   189→
   190→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   191→
   192→    requested_tier = (
   193→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   194→    )
   195→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   196→    fallback_reason = None
   197→    filtered = all_items
   198→
   199→    if requested_tier is not None:
   200→        filtered = [
   201→            item
   202→            for item in all_items
   203→            if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == requested_tier
   204→        ]
   205→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   206→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   207→            if chosen is not None:
   208→                selected_tier = chosen
   209→                filtered = [
   210→                    item
   211→                    for item in all_items
   212→                    if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == chosen
   213→                ]
   214→                fallback_reason = (
   215→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   216→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   217→                )
   218→        elif not filtered:
   219→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   220→
   221→    total = len(filtered)
   222→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   223→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   224→
   225→    if resolved_options.explain:
   226→        for item in filtered:
   227→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   228→
   229→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   230→    return {
   231→        "items": filtered,
   232→        "total": total,
   233→        "tier_counts": counts,
   234→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   235→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   236→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   237→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   238→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   239→    }
   240→
   241→
   242→__all__ = [
   243→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   244→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   245→    "WorkQueueResult",
   246→    "build_work_queue",
   247→    "group_queue_items",
   248→]
   249→

1→"""Scorecard dimension row helpers for the engine/planning layer.
     2→
     3→Provides ``scorecard_dimension_rows`` without importing app-layer modules.
     4→The app scorecard surface re-exports this so external call-sites are unaffected.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from desloppify.scoring import DIMENSIONS
    10→
    11→
    12→def scorecard_dimension_rows(
    13→    state: dict,
    14→    *,
    15→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    16→) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
    17→    """Return scorecard rows using canonical dimension ordering.
    18→
    19→    Tries ``prepare_scorecard_dimensions`` from the app layer when available
    20→    (deferred import to avoid hard engine -> app dependency), then falls back
    21→    to a simple mechanical-dimension listing.
    22→    """
    23→    if dim_scores is None:
    24→        dim_scores = (
    25→            state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if isinstance(state, dict) else {}
    26→        )
    27→        projected_state = state
    28→    else:
    29→        projected_state = dict(state)
    30→        projected_state["dimension_scores"] = dim_scores
    31→
    32→    # Try the full app-layer scorecard projection when it can be imported.
    33→    try:
    34→        from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimensions import (
    35→            prepare_scorecard_dimensions,
    36→        )
    37→
    38→        rows = prepare_scorecard_dimensions(projected_state)
    39→        if rows:
    40→            return rows
    41→    except ImportError:
    42→        pass
    43→
    44→    # Fallback for synthetic/unit-test states without full scorecard context.
    45→    fallback_dim_scores = dim_scores or {}
    46→    if not isinstance(fallback_dim_scores, dict):
    47→        return []
    48→
    49→    mechanical_names = [dimension.name for dimension in DIMENSIONS]
    50→    fallback_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
    51→    for name in mechanical_names:
    52→        data = fallback_dim_scores.get(name)
    53→        if isinstance(data, dict):
    54→            fallback_rows.append((name, data))
    55→    fallback_rows.extend(
    56→        sorted(
    57→            [
    58→                (name, data)
    59→                for name, data in fallback_dim_scores.items()
    60→                if name not in mechanical_names and isinstance(data, dict)
    61→            ],
    62→            key=lambda item: item[0].lower(),
    63→        )
    64→    )
    65→    return fallback_rows
    66→

   303→def compute_score_bundle(

   306→    *,
   307→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   308→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   309→) -> ScoreBundle:
   310→    """Compute all score channels from one scoring engine pass."""
   311→    by_mode = compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   312→        findings,
   313→        potentials,
   314→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   315→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   316→    )
   317→
   318→    lenient_scores = by_mode["lenient"]
   319→    strict_scores = by_mode["strict"]
   320→    verified_strict_scores = by_mode["verified_strict"]
   321→
   322→    mechanical_lenient_scores = {
   323→        name: data
   324→        for name, data in lenient_scores.items()
   325→        if "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})
   326→    }
   327→
   328→    return ScoreBundle(
   329→        dimension_scores=lenient_scores,
   330→        strict_dimension_scores=strict_scores,
   331→        verified_strict_dimension_scores=verified_strict_scores,
   332→        overall_score=compute_health_score(lenient_scores),
   333→        objective_score=compute_health_score(mechanical_lenient_scores),
   334→        strict_score=compute_health_score(strict_scores),
   335→        verified_strict_score=compute_health_score(verified_strict_scores),
   336→    )
   337→
   338→
   339→def compute_score_impact(
   340→    dimension_scores: dict,
   341→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   342→    detector: str,
   343→    issues_to_fix: int,
   344→) -> float:
   345→    """Estimate score improvement from fixing N issues in a detector."""
   346→    target_dim = None
   347→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   348→        if detector in dim.detectors:
   349→            target_dim = dim
   350→            break
   351→    if target_dim is None or target_dim.name not in dimension_scores:
   352→        return 0.0
   353→
   354→    potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
   355→    if potential <= 0:
   356→        return 0.0
   357→
   358→    dim_data = dimension_scores[target_dim.name]
   359→    old_score = compute_health_score(dimension_scores)
   360→
   361→    det_data = dim_data["detectors"].get(detector)
   362→    if not det_data:
   363→        return 0.0
   364→
   365→    old_weighted = det_data["weighted_failures"]
   366→    new_weighted = max(0.0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix * 1.0)
   367→
   368→    total_potential = 0
   369→    total_new_weighted_failures = 0.0
   370→    for det in target_dim.detectors:
   371→        det_values = dim_data["detectors"].get(det)
   372→        if not det_values:
   373→            continue
   374→        total_potential += det_values["potential"]
   375→        if det == detector:
   376→            total_new_weighted_failures += new_weighted
   377→        else:
   378→            total_new_weighted_failures += det_values["weighted_failures"]
   379→    if total_potential <= 0:
   380→        return 0.0
   381→
   382→    new_dim_score = (
   383→        max(
   384→            0.0,
   385→            (total_potential - total_new_weighted_failures) / total_potential,
   386→        )
   387→        * 100
   388→    )
   389→
   390→    simulated = {k: dict(v) for k, v in dimension_scores.items()}
   391→    simulated[target_dim.name] = {**dim_data, "score": round(new_dim_score, 1)}
   392→    new_score = compute_health_score(simulated)
   393→
   394→    return round(new_score - old_score, 1)
   395→
   396→
   397→def get_dimension_for_detector(detector: str) -> Dimension | None:
   398→    """Look up which dimension a detector belongs to."""
   399→    policy = DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(detector)
   400→    if not policy or policy.dimension is None:
   401→        return None
   402→    return DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.get(policy.dimension)
   403→
   404→
   405→__all__ = [
   406→    "ScoreBundle",
   407→    "compute_dimension_scores_by_mode",
   408→    "compute_dimension_scores",
   409→    "compute_health_breakdown",
   410→    "compute_health_score",
   411→    "compute_score_bundle",
   412→    "compute_score_impact",
   413→    "get_dimension_for_detector",
   414→]
   415→

1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   169→
   170→
   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:
   174→        return {}
   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:
   179→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   180→        if not name:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
