```
   186→def utc_now() -> str:
   187→    """Return current UTC timestamp with second-level precision."""
   188→    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   189→
   190→
   191→def empty_state() -> StateModel:
   192→    """Return a new empty state payload."""
   193→    return {
   194→        "version": CURRENT_VERSION,
   195→        "created": utc_now(),
   196→        "last_scan": None,
   197→        "scan_count": 0,
   198→        "overall_score": 0,
   199→        "objective_score": 0,
   200→        "strict_score": 0,
   201→        "verified_strict_score": 0,
   202→        "stats": {},
   203→        "findings": {},
   204→        "scan_coverage": {},
   205→        "score_confidence": {},
   206→        "subjective_integrity": {},
   207→        "subjective_assessments": {},
   208→    }
   209→
   210→
   211→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   212→    try:
   213→        parsed = int(value)
   214→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   215→        return default
   216→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   217→
   218→
   219→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   220→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   221→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   222→        state.setdefault(key, value)
   223→
   224→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   225→        state["findings"] = {}
   226→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   227→        state["stats"] = {}
   228→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
   229→        state["scan_history"] = []
   230→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
   231→        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
   232→    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
   233→        state["score_confidence"] = {}
   234→    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
   235→        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}
   236→
   237→    findings = state["findings"]
   238→    to_remove: list[str] = []
   239→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   240→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   241→            to_remove.append(finding_id)
   242→            continue
   243→
   244→        finding.setdefault("id", finding_id)
   245→        finding.setdefault("detector", "unknown")
   246→        finding.setdefault("file", "")
   247→        finding.setdefault("tier", 3)
   248→        finding.setdefault("confidence", "low")
   249→        finding.setdefault("summary", "")
   250→        finding.setdefault("detail", {})
   251→        finding.setdefault("status", "open")
   252→        finding["status"] = canonical_finding_status(
   253→            finding.get("status"),
   254→            default="open",
   255→        )
   256→        finding.setdefault("note", None)
   257→        finding.setdefault("first_seen", state.get("created") or utc_now())
   258→        finding.setdefault("last_seen", finding["first_seen"])
   259→        finding.setdefault("resolved_at", None)
   260→        finding["reopen_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(
   261→            finding.get("reopen_count", 0), default=0
   262→        )
   263→        finding.setdefault("suppressed", False)
   264→        finding.setdefault("suppressed_at", None)
   265→        finding.setdefault("suppression_pattern", None)
   266→
   267→    for finding_id in to_remove:
   268→        findings.pop(finding_id, None)
   269→
   270→    for entry in state["scan_history"]:
   271→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   272→            continue
   273→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
   274→        if integrity is not None and not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   275→            entry["subjective_integrity"] = None
   276→
   277→    state["scan_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count", 0), default=0)
   278→    return None
   279→
   280→
   281→def validate_state_invariants(state: StateModel) -> None:
   282→    """Raise ValueError when core state invariants are violated."""
   283→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   284→        raise ValueError("state.findings must be a dict")
   285→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   286→        raise ValueError("state.stats must be a dict")
   287→
   288→    findings = state["findings"]
   289→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   290→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   291→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} must be a dict")
   292→        if finding.get("id") != finding_id:
   293→            raise ValueError(f"finding id mismatch for {finding_id!r}")
   294→        if finding.get("status") not in _ALLOWED_FINDING_STATUSES:
   295→            raise ValueError(
   296→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid status {finding.get('status')!r}"
   297→            )
   298→
   299→        tier = finding.get("tier")
   300→        if not isinstance(tier, int) or tier < 1 or tier > 4:
   301→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid tier {tier!r}")
   302→
   303→        reopen_count = finding.get("reopen_count")
   304→        if not isinstance(reopen_count, int) or reopen_count < 0:
   305→            raise ValueError(
   306→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid reopen_count {reopen_count!r}"
   307→            )
   308→
   309→
   310→def json_default(obj: Any) -> Any:
   311→    """JSON serializer that handles known types and rejects unknowns."""
   312→    if isinstance(obj, set):
   313→        return sorted(obj)
   314→    if isinstance(obj, Path):
   315→        return str(obj).replace("\\", "/")
   316→    if hasattr(obj, "isoformat"):
   317→        return obj.isoformat()
   318→    raise TypeError(
   319→        f"Object of type {type(obj).__name__} is not JSON serializable: {obj!r}"
   320→    )
   321→
   322→
   323→def get_overall_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   324→    """Canonical overall score (lenient, includes subjective dimensions)."""
   325→    return state.get("overall_score")
   326→
   327→
   328→def get_objective_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   329→    """Canonical objective score (lenient, mechanical dimensions only)."""
   330→    return state.get("objective_score")
   331→
   332→
   333→def get_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   334→    """Canonical strict score (strict, includes subjective dimensions)."""
   335→    return state.get("strict_score")
   336→
   337→
   338→def get_verified_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   339→    """Strict score that only credits scan-verified fixes.
   340→
   341→    Returns None if no scan-verified score exists yet (fresh state or
   342→    no scan has run). Does not fall back to the unverified strict_score.
   343→    """
   344→    return state.get("verified_strict_score")
   345→
```

```
1→"""Dimension and overall scoring aggregation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
     8→from desloppify.engine._scoring.detection import detector_stats_by_mode
     9→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
    10→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES,
    11→    DIMENSIONS,
    12→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME,
    13→    FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE,
    14→    MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
    15→    MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
    16→    MIN_SAMPLE,
    17→    SCORING_MODES,
    18→    SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
    19→    SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
    20→    Dimension,
    21→    ScoreMode,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import (
    24→    append_subjective_dimensions,
    25→)
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29→class ScoreBundle:
    30→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    31→    strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    32→    verified_strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    33→    overall_score: float
    34→    objective_score: float
    35→    strict_score: float
    36→    verified_strict_score: float
    37→
    38→
    39→def compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
    40→    findings: dict,
    41→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    42→    *,
    43→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
    44→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    45→) -> dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]]:
    46→    """Compute dimension scores for lenient/strict/verified_strict in one pass."""
    47→    results: dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]] = {mode: {} for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    48→
    49→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    50→        totals = {
    51→            mode: {
    52→                "checks": 0,
    53→                "issues": 0,
    54→                "weighted_failures": 0.0,
    55→                "detectors": {},
    56→            }
    57→            for mode in SCORING_MODES
    58→        }
    59→
    60→        for detector in dim.detectors:
    61→            potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    62→            if potential <= 0:
    63→                continue
    64→
    65→            detector_stats = detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)
    66→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    67→                pass_rate, issues, weighted = detector_stats[mode]
    68→                totals[mode]["checks"] += potential
    69→                totals[mode]["issues"] += issues
    70→                totals[mode]["weighted_failures"] += weighted
    71→                totals[mode]["detectors"][detector] = {
    72→                    "potential": potential,
    73→                    "pass_rate": pass_rate,
    74→                    "issues": issues,
    75→                    "weighted_failures": weighted,
    76→                }
    77→
    78→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    79→            total_checks = totals[mode]["checks"]
    80→            if total_checks <= 0:
    81→                continue
    82→            dim_score = (
    83→                max(
    84→                    0.0,
    85→                    (total_checks - totals[mode]["weighted_failures"]) / total_checks,
    86→                )
    87→                * 100
    88→            )
    89→            results[mode][dim.name] = {
    90→                "score": round(dim_score, 1),
    91→                "tier": dim.tier,
    92→                "checks": total_checks,
    93→                "issues": totals[mode]["issues"],
    94→                "detectors": totals[mode]["detectors"],
    95→            }
    96→
    97→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    98→        append_subjective_dimensions(
    99→            results[mode],
   100→            findings,
   101→            subjective_assessments,
   102→            FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode],
   103→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   104→        )
   105→    return results
   106→
   107→
   108→def compute_dimension_scores(
   109→    findings: dict,
   110→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   111→    *,
   112→    strict: bool = False,
   113→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   114→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   115→) -> dict[str, dict]:
   116→    """Compute per-dimension scores from findings and potentials."""
   117→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   118→    return compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   119→        findings,
   120→        potentials,
   121→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   122→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   123→    )[mode]
   124→
   125→
   126→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
   127→    return " ".join(str(name).strip().lower().split())
   128→
   129→
   130→def _mechanical_dimension_weight(name: str) -> float:
   131→    return float(
   132→        MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
   133→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
   134→            1.0,
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→
   138→
   139→def _subjective_dimension_weight(name: str, data: dict) -> float:
   140→    subjective_meta = (
   141→        data.get("detectors", {}).get("subjective_assessment", {})
   142→        if isinstance(data, dict)
   143→        else {}
   144→    )
   145→    configured = (
   146→        subjective_meta.get("configured_weight")
   147→        if isinstance(subjective_meta, dict)
   148→        else None
   149→    )
   150→    if is_numeric(configured):
   151→        return max(0.0, float(configured))
   152→
   153→    return float(
   154→        SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
   155→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
   156→            1.0,
   157→        )
   158→    )
   159→
   160→
   161→def compute_health_breakdown(
   162→    dimension_scores: dict, *, score_key: str = "score"
   163→) -> dict[str, object]:
   164→    """Return pool averages and weighted contribution breakdown for score transparency."""
   165→    if not dimension_scores:
   166→        return {
   167→            "overall_score": 100.0,
   168→            "mechanical_fraction": 1.0,
   169→            "subjective_fraction": 0.0,
   170→            "mechanical_avg": 100.0,
   171→            "subjective_avg": None,
   172→            "entries": [],
   173→        }
   174→
   175→    mech_sum = 0.0
   176→    mech_weight = 0.0
   177→    subj_sum = 0.0
   178→    subj_weight = 0.0
   179→    mechanical_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   180→    subjective_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   181→
   182→    for name, data in dimension_scores.items():
   183→        score = float(data.get(score_key, data.get("score", 0.0)))
   184→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
   185→        if is_subjective:
   186→            configured = max(0.0, _subjective_dimension_weight(name, data))
   187→            effective = configured
   188→            subj_sum += score * effective
   189→            subj_weight += effective
   190→            subjective_rows.append(
   191→                {
   192→                    "name": str(name),
   193→                    "score": score,
   194→                    "configured_weight": configured,
   195→                    "effective_weight": effective,
   196→                }
   197→            )
   198→            continue
   199→
   200→        checks = float(data.get("checks", 0) or 0)
   201→        sample_factor = min(1.0, checks / MIN_SAMPLE) if checks > 0 else 0.0
   202→        configured = max(0.0, _mechanical_dimension_weight(name))
   203→        effective = configured * sample_factor
   204→        mech_sum += score * effective
   205→        mech_weight += effective
   206→        mechanical_rows.append(
   207→            {
   208→                "name": str(name),
   209→                "score": score,
   210→                "checks": checks,
   211→                "sample_factor": sample_factor,
   212→                "configured_weight": configured,
   213→                "effective_weight": effective,
   214→            }
   215→        )
   216→
   217→    mech_avg = (mech_sum / mech_weight) if mech_weight > 0 else 100.0
   218→    subj_avg = (subj_sum / subj_weight) if subj_weight > 0 else None
   219→
   220→    if subj_avg is None:
   221→        mechanical_fraction = 1.0
   222→        subjective_fraction = 0.0
   223→        overall_score = round(mech_avg, 1)
   224→    elif mech_weight == 0:
   225→        mechanical_fraction = 0.0
   226→        subjective_fraction = 1.0
   227→        overall_score = round(subj_avg, 1)
   228→    else:
   229→        mechanical_fraction = MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   230→        subjective_fraction = SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   231→        overall_score = round(
   232→            mech_avg * mechanical_fraction + subj_avg * subjective_fraction,
   233→            1,
   234→        )
   235→
   236→    entries: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   237→
   238→    for row in mechanical_rows:
   239→        pool_share = (
   240→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / mech_weight if mech_weight > 0 else 0.0
   241→        )
   242→        per_point = mechanical_fraction * pool_share
   243→        score = float(row["score"])
   244→        entries.append(
   245→            {
   246→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   247→                "pool": "mechanical",
   248→                "score": score,
   249→                "checks": float(row["checks"]),
   250→                "sample_factor": float(row["sample_factor"]),
   251→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   252→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   253→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   254→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   255→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   256→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   257→            }
   258→        )
   259→
   260→    for row in subjective_rows:
   261→        pool_share = (
   262→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / subj_weight if subj_weight > 0 else 0.0
   263→        )
   264→        per_point = subjective_fraction * pool_share
   265→        score = float(row["score"])
   266→        entries.append(
   267→            {
   268→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   269→                "pool": "subjective",
   270→                "score": score,
   271→                "checks": 0.0,
   272→                "sample_factor": 1.0,
   273→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   274→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   275→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   276→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   277→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   278→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   279→            }
   280→        )
   281→
   282→    return {
   283→        "overall_score": overall_score,
   284→        "mechanical_fraction": mechanical_fraction,
   285→        "subjective_fraction": subjective_fraction,
   286→        "mechanical_avg": mech_avg,
   287→        "subjective_avg": subj_avg,
   288→        "entries": entries,
   289→    }
   290→
   291→
   292→def compute_health_score(
   293→    dimension_scores: dict, *, score_key: str = "score"
   294→) -> float:
   295→    """Budget-weighted blend of mechanical and subjective dimension scores."""
   296→    return float(
   297→        compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores, score_key=score_key)[
   298→            "overall_score"
   299→        ]
   300→    )
   301→
   302→
   303→def compute_score_bundle(
   304→    findings: dict,
   305→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   306→    *,
   307→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   308→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   309→) -> ScoreBundle:
   310→    """Compute all score channels from one scoring engine pass."""
   311→    by_mode = compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   312→        findings,
   313→        potentials,
   314→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   315→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   316→    )
   317→
   318→    lenient_scores = by_mode["lenient"]
   319→    strict_scores = by_mode["strict"]
   320→    verified_strict_scores = by_mode["verified_strict"]
   321→
   322→    mechanical_lenient_scores = {
   323→        name: data
   324→        for name, data in lenient_scores.items()
   325→        if "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})
   326→    }
   327→
   328→    return ScoreBundle(
   329→        dimension_scores=lenient_scores,
   330→        strict_dimension_scores=strict_scores,
   331→        verified_strict_dimension_scores=verified_strict_scores,
   332→        overall_score=compute_health_score(lenient_scores),
   333→        objective_score=compute_health_score(mechanical_lenient_scores),
   334→        strict_score=compute_health_score(strict_scores),
   335→        verified_strict_score=compute_health_score(verified_strict_scores),
   336→    )
   337→
   338→
   339→def compute_score_impact(
   340→    dimension_scores: dict,
   341→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   342→    detector: str,
   343→    issues_to_fix: int,
   344→) -> float:
   345→    """Estimate score improvement from fixing N issues in a detector."""
   346→    target_dim = None
   347→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   348→        if detector in dim.detectors:
   349→            target_dim = dim
   350→            break
   351→    if target_dim is None or target_dim.name not in dimension_scores:
   352→        return 0.0
   353→
   354→    potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
   355→    if potential <= 0:
   356→        return 0.0
   357→
   358→    dim_data = dimension_scores[target_dim.name]
   359→    old_score = compute_health_score(dimension_scores)
   360→
   361→    det_data = dim_data["detectors"].get(detector)
   362→    if not det_data:
   363→        return 0.0
   364→
   365→    old_weighted = det_data["weighted_failures"]
   366→    new_weighted = max(0.0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix * 1.0)
   367→
   368→    total_potential = 0
   369→    total_new_weighted_failures = 0.0
   370→    for det in target_dim.detectors:
   371→        det_values = dim_data["detectors"].get(det)
   372→        if not det_values:
   373→            continue
   374→        total_potential += det_values["potential"]
   375→        if det == detector:
   376→            total_new_weighted_failures += new_weighted
   377→        else:
   378→            total_new_weighted_failures += det_values["weighted_failures"]
   379→    if total_potential <= 0:
   380→        return 0.0
   381→
   382→    new_dim_score = (
   383→        max(
   384→            0.0,
   385→            (total_potential - total_new_weighted_failures) / total_potential,
   386→        )
   387→        * 100
   388→    )
   389→
   390→    simulated = {k: dict(v) for k, v in dimension_scores.items()}
   391→    simulated[target_dim.name] = {**dim_data, "score": round(new_dim_score, 1)}
   392→    new_score = compute_health_score(simulated)
   393→
   394→    return round(new_score - old_score, 1)
   395→
   396→
   397→def get_dimension_for_detector(detector: str) -> Dimension | None:
   398→    """Look up which dimension a detector belongs to."""
   399→    policy = DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(detector)
   400→    if not policy or policy.dimension is None:
   401→        return None
   402→    return DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.get(policy.dimension)
   403→
   404→
   405→__all__ = [
   406→    "ScoreBundle",
   407→    "compute_dimension_scores_by_mode",
   408→    "compute_dimension_scores",
   409→    "compute_health_breakdown",
   410→    "compute_health_score",
   411→    "compute_score_bundle",
   412→    "compute_score_impact",
   413→    "get_dimension_for_detector",
   414→]
   415→
```

```
    40→
    41→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    42→    """Show score dashboard."""
    43→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    44→    state = runtime.state
    45→    config = runtime.config
    46→
    47→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
    48→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    49→    scorecard_dims = scorecard_dimensions_payload(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    50→    subjective_measures = [row for row in scorecard_dims if row.get("subjective")]
    51→    suppression = state_mod.suppression_metrics(state)
    52→
    53→    if getattr(args, "json", False):
    54→        print(
    55→            json.dumps(
    56→                _status_json_payload(
    57→                    state,
    58→                    stats,
    59→                    dim_scores,
    60→                    scorecard_dims,
    61→                    subjective_measures,
    62→                    suppression,
    63→                ),
    64→                indent=2,
    65→            )
    66→        )
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    73→    if stale_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    76→    if skill_warning:
    77→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    78→
    79→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    80→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
    81→    target_strict_score = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    82→
    83→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    84→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    85→    narrative = compute_narrative(
    86→        state,
    87→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="status"),
    88→    )
    89→    ignores = config.get("ignore", [])
    90→
    91→    for line, style in score_summary_lines(
    92→        overall_score=scores.overall,
    93→        objective_score=scores.objective,
    94→        strict_score=scores.strict,
    95→        verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
    96→    ):
    97→        print(colorize(line, style))
    98→    print_scan_metrics(state)
    99→    print_open_scope_breakdown(state)
   100→    print_scan_completeness(state)
   101→
   102→    if dim_scores:
   103→        show_dimension_table(state, dim_scores)
   104→        reporting_dimensions_mod.show_score_model_breakdown(
   105→            state,
   106→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
   107→        )
   108→    else:
   109→        show_tier_progress_table(by_tier)
   110→
   111→    # Load living plan for plan-aware rendering
   112→    try:
   113→        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   114→        _plan = load_plan()
   115→        _plan_active = _plan if (
   116→            _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
   117→        ) else None
   118→    except Exception:
   119→        _plan_active = None
   120→
   121→    if dim_scores:
   122→        show_focus_suggestion(dim_scores, state, plan=_plan_active)
   123→        show_subjective_followup(
   124→            state,
   125→            dim_scores,
   126→            target_strict_score=target_strict_score,
   127→        )
   128→
   129→    show_review_summary(state)
   130→    show_structural_areas(state)
   131→    show_agent_plan(narrative, plan=_plan_active)
   132→

   135→        print()
   136→
   137→    if ignores:
   138→        show_ignore_summary(ignores, suppression)
   139→
   140→    review_age = config.get("review_max_age_days", 30)
   141→    if review_age != 30:
   142→        label = "never" if review_age == 0 else f"{review_age} days"
   143→        print(colorize(f"  Review staleness: {label}", "dim"))
   144→    print()
   145→
   146→    write_status_query(
   147→        state=state,
   148→        stats=stats,
   149→        by_tier=by_tier,
   150→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   151→        scorecard_dims=scorecard_dims,
   152→        subjective_measures=subjective_measures,
   153→        suppression=suppression,
   154→        narrative=narrative,
   155→        ignores=ignores,
   156→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   157→        objective_score=scores.objective,
   158→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   159→        verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
   160→        plan=_plan_active,
   161→    )

   193→        "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   194→        "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   195→    }
```

```
    37→
    38→
    39→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    40→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    41→    try:
    42→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    43→    except OSError as exc:
    44→        print_error(f"could not save state: {exc}")
    45→        sys.exit(1)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    49→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    50→    try:
    51→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    52→    except OSError as exc:
    53→        print_error(f"could not save config: {exc}")
    54→        sys.exit(1)
    55→
    56→
    57→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    58→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    59→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    60→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    61→
    62→    state_file = state_path(args)
    63→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    64→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    65→        state,
    66→        args,
    67→        attestation=attestation,
    68→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    69→    )
    70→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    71→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    72→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    73→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    74→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
    78→    if not all_resolved:
    79→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    80→        print(
    81→            colorize(
    82→                f"No {status_label} findings matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}",
    83→                "yellow",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→        return
    87→
    88→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    89→
    90→    # Remove resolved items from the living plan queue
    91→    try:
    92→        from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan, purge_ids, save_plan
    93→
    94→        if has_living_plan():
    95→            plan = load_plan()
    96→            purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    97→            if purged:
    98→                save_plan(plan)
    99→                print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
   100→    except Exception:
   101→        pass
   102→
   103→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   104→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   105→        state,
   106→        status=args.status,
   107→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   108→    )
   109→    has_review_findings = any(
   110→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   111→        for fid in all_resolved
   112→    )
   113→    _print_score_movement(
   114→        status=args.status,
   115→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   116→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   117→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   118→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   119→        state=state,
   120→        has_review_findings=has_review_findings,
   121→    )
   122→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   123→        args=args,
   124→        state=state,
   125→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   126→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   127→    )
   128→
   129→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   130→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   131→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   132→        state,
   133→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   134→    )
   135→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   136→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   137→
   138→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   139→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   140→        ResolveQueryContext(
   141→            patterns=args.patterns,
   142→            status=args.status,
   143→            resolved=all_resolved,
   144→            next_command=next_command,
   145→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   146→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   147→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   148→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   149→            attestation=attestation,
   150→            narrative=narrative,
   151→            state=state,
   152→        )
   153→    )
   154→
   155→
   156→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   157→    """Add a pattern to the ignore list."""
   158→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   159→    if not _validate_attestation(attestation):
   160→        _show_attestation_requirement("Ignore", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   161→        sys.exit(1)
   162→
   163→    state_file = state_path(args)
   164→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   165→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   166→
   167→    config = command_runtime(args).config
   168→    config_mod.add_ignore_pattern(config, args.pattern)
   169→    _save_config_or_exit(config)
   170→
   171→    removed = state_mod.remove_ignored_findings(state, args.pattern)
   172→    state.setdefault("attestation_log", []).append(
   173→        {
   174→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
   175→            "command": "ignore",
   176→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   177→            "attestation": attestation,
   178→            "affected": removed,
   179→        }
   180→    )
   181→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   182→
   183→    print(colorize(f"Added ignore pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
   184→    if removed:
   185→        print(f"  Removed {removed} matching findings from state.")
   186→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   187→
   188→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   189→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   190→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   191→        state,
   192→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="ignore"),
   193→    )
   194→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   195→    write_query(
   196→        {
   197→            "command": "ignore",
   198→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   199→            "removed": removed,
   200→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
   201→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
   202→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
   203→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   204→            "attestation": attestation,
   205→            "narrative": narrative,
   206→        }
   207→    )
   208→
```

```
1→"""Finding upsert/auto-resolve helpers for scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import matched_ignore_pattern
     6→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import matches_exclusion
     7→
     8→
     9→def find_suspect_detectors(
    10→    existing: dict,
    11→    current_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    12→    force_resolve: bool,
    13→    ran_detectors: set[str] | None = None,
    14→) -> set[str]:
    15→    """Detectors that had open findings but likely did not actually run this scan."""
    16→    if force_resolve:
    17→        return set()
    18→
    19→    previous_open_by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
    20→    for finding in existing.values():
    21→        if finding["status"] != "open":
    22→            continue
    23→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    24→        previous_open_by_detector[detector] = (
    25→            previous_open_by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
    26→        )
    27→
    28→    # 'review' findings enter via `desloppify review --import`, not via scan phases.
    29→    # They are always marked suspect so the scan never auto-resolves them.
    30→    import_only_detectors = {"review"}
    31→    suspect: set[str] = set()
    32→
    33→    for detector, previous_count in previous_open_by_detector.items():
    34→        if detector in import_only_detectors:
    35→            suspect.add(detector)
    36→            continue
    37→        if current_by_detector.get(detector, 0) > 0:
    38→            continue
    39→        if ran_detectors is not None:
    40→            if detector not in ran_detectors:
    41→                suspect.add(detector)
    42→            continue
    43→        if previous_count >= 3:
    44→            suspect.add(detector)
    45→
    46→    return suspect
    47→
    48→
    49→def auto_resolve_disappeared(
    50→    existing: dict,
    51→    current_ids: set[str],
    52→    suspect_detectors: set[str],
    53→    now: str,
    54→    *,
    55→    lang: str | None,
    56→    scan_path: str | None,
    57→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    58→) -> tuple[int, int, int, set[str]]:
    59→    """Auto-resolve open/wontfix/fixed/false_positive findings absent from scan.
    60→
    61→    Returns (resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolved_detectors).
    62→    """
    63→    resolved = skipped_other_lang = skipped_out_of_scope = 0
    64→    resolved_detectors: set[str] = set()
    65→
    66→    for finding_id, previous in existing.items():
    67→        if finding_id in current_ids or previous["status"] not in (
    68→            "open",
    69→            "wontfix",
    70→            "fixed",
    71→            "false_positive",
    72→        ):
    73→            continue
    74→
    75→        if lang and previous.get("lang") and previous["lang"] != lang:
    76→            skipped_other_lang += 1
    77→            continue
    78→
    79→        if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
    80→            prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    81→            if (
    82→                not previous["file"].startswith(prefix)
    83→                and previous["file"] != scan_path
    84→            ):
    85→                skipped_out_of_scope += 1
    86→                continue
    87→
    88→        if exclude and any(matches_exclusion(previous["file"], ex) for ex in exclude):
    89→            continue
    90→
    91→        if previous.get("detector", "unknown") in suspect_detectors:
    92→            continue
    93→
    94→        previous_status = previous["status"]
    95→        previous["status"] = "auto_resolved"
    96→        previous["resolved_at"] = now
    97→        previous["suppressed"] = False
    98→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
    99→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
   100→        previous["resolution_attestation"] = {
   101→            "kind": "scan_verified",
   102→            "text": "Disappeared from detector output",
   103→            "attested_at": now,
   104→            "scan_verified": True,
   105→        }
   106→        previous["note"] = (
   107→            "Fixed despite wontfix — disappeared from scan (was wontfix)"
   108→            if previous_status == "wontfix"
   109→            else "Disappeared from scan — likely fixed"
   110→        )
   111→        resolved_detectors.add(previous.get("detector", "unknown"))
   112→        resolved += 1
   113→
   114→    return resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolved_detectors
   115→
   116→
   117→def upsert_findings(
   118→    existing: dict,
   119→    current_findings: list[dict],
   120→    ignore: list[str],
   121→    now: str,
   122→    *,
   123→    lang: str | None,
   124→) -> tuple[set[str], int, int, dict[str, int], int, set[str]]:
   125→    """Insert new findings and update existing ones.
   126→
   127→    Returns (current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors).
   128→    """
   129→    current_ids: set[str] = set()
   130→    new_count = reopened_count = ignored_count = 0
   131→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   132→    changed_detectors: set[str] = set()
   133→
   134→    for finding in current_findings:
   135→        finding_id = finding["id"]
   136→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
   137→        current_ids.add(finding_id)
   138→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   139→        matched_ignore = matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, finding["file"], ignore)
   140→        if matched_ignore:
   141→            ignored_count += 1
   142→
   143→        if lang:
   144→            finding["lang"] = lang
   145→
   146→        if finding_id not in existing:
   147→            existing[finding_id] = dict(finding)
   148→            if matched_ignore:
   149→                existing[finding_id]["suppressed"] = True
   150→                existing[finding_id]["suppressed_at"] = now
   151→                existing[finding_id]["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   152→                continue
   153→            new_count += 1
   154→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   155→            continue
   156→
   157→        previous = existing[finding_id]
   158→        previous.update(
   159→            last_seen=now,
   160→            tier=finding["tier"],
   161→            confidence=finding["confidence"],
   162→            summary=finding["summary"],
   163→            detail=finding.get("detail", {}),
   164→        )
   165→        if "zone" in finding:
   166→            previous["zone"] = finding["zone"]
   167→        if lang and not previous.get("lang"):
   168→            previous["lang"] = lang
   169→
   170→        if matched_ignore:
   171→            previous["suppressed"] = True
   172→            previous["suppressed_at"] = now
   173→            previous["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   174→            if previous["status"] in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"):
   175→                previous["status"] = "open"
   176→                previous["resolved_at"] = None
   177→                previous["note"] = (
   178→                    "Suppressed by ignore pattern — remains unresolved for score integrity"
   179→                )
   180→            continue
   181→
   182→        previous["suppressed"] = False
   183→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
   184→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
   185→
   186→        if previous["status"] in ("fixed", "auto_resolved"):
   187→            # subjective_review findings are condition-based.  When just
   188→            # auto-resolved by an agent import, skip reopening to avoid a
   189→            # resolve-then-reopen loop on the same scan cycle.
   190→            if (
   191→                detector == "subjective_review"
   192→                and previous["status"] == "auto_resolved"
   193→                and (previous.get("resolution_attestation") or {}).get("kind") == "agent_import"
   194→            ):
   195→                continue
   196→            previous_status = previous["status"]
   197→            previous["reopen_count"] = previous.get("reopen_count", 0) + 1
   198→            previous.pop("resolution_attestation", None)
   199→            previous.update(
   200→                status="open",
   201→                resolved_at=None,
   202→                note=(
   203→                    f"Reopened (×{previous['reopen_count']}) "
   204→                    f"— reappeared in scan (was {previous_status})"
   205→                ),
   206→            )
   207→            reopened_count += 1
   208→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   209→
   210→    return current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors
   211→
   212→
   213→__all__ = [
   214→    "auto_resolve_disappeared",
   215→    "find_suspect_detectors",
   216→    "upsert_findings",
   217→]
   218→
```

```
95→def unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    96→    """Return subjective dimension display names that are still 0% placeholders."""
    97→    if not dim_scores:
    98→        return []
    99→
   100→    unassessed: list[str] = []
   101→    for name, info in dim_scores.items():
   102→        detectors = info.get("detectors", {})
   103→        if "subjective_assessment" not in detectors:
   104→            continue
   105→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
   106→        if isinstance(assessment_meta, dict) and assessment_meta.get("placeholder"):
   107→            unassessed.append(name)
   108→            continue
   109→        strict_val = float(info.get("strict", info.get("score", 100.0)))
   110→        issues = int(info.get("issues", 0))
   111→        if strict_val <= 0.0 and issues == 0:
   112→            unassessed.append(name)
   113→
   114→    unassessed.sort()
   115→    return unassessed
   116→
```

```
    18→_SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD = 2

    20→
    21→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    22→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    23→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    24→    if isinstance(history, list):
    25→        for entry in reversed(history):
    26→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    28→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    29→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    30→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    31→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    32→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    33→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    34→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    35→    return None
    36→
    37→
    38→def _count_findings(findings: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    39→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    40→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    41→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    42→
    43→    for finding in findings.values():
    44→        status = finding["status"]
    45→        tier = finding.get("tier", 3)
    46→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    47→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    48→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    49→
    50→    return counters, tier_stats
    51→
    52→
    53→def _coerce_subjective_score(value: dict | float | int | str | None) -> float:
    54→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float."""
    55→    raw = value.get("score", 0) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
    56→    try:
    57→        score = float(raw)
    58→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    59→        score = 0.0
    60→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, score))
    61→
    62→
    63→def _subjective_target_matches(
    64→    subjective_assessments: dict, *, target: float
    65→) -> list[str]:
    66→    """Return dimension keys whose subjective score matches the target band."""
    67→    matches = [
    68→        dimension
    69→        for dimension, payload in subjective_assessments.items()
    70→        if matches_target_score(_coerce_subjective_score(payload), target)
    71→    ]
    72→    return sorted(matches)
    73→
    74→
    75→def _subjective_integrity_baseline(target: float | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    76→    """Create baseline subjective-integrity metadata for scan/reporting output."""
    77→    return {
    78→        "status": "disabled" if target is None else "pass",
    79→        "target_score": None if target is None else round(float(target), 2),
    80→        "matched_count": 0,
    81→        "matched_dimensions": [],
    82→        "reset_dimensions": [],
    83→    }
    84→
    85→

    87→    subjective_assessments: dict,

    90→) -> tuple[dict, dict[str, object]]:
    91→    """Apply anti-gaming penalties for subjective scores clustered on the target."""

    97→    meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(normalized_target)

    99→    meta["matched_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions

   102→        meta["status"] = "warn" if matched_dimensions else "pass"
   103→        return subjective_assessments, meta
```

```
    33→
    34→# Mechanical detectors → subjective dimensions they provide evidence for.
    35→# When findings from these detectors change during a scan, the corresponding
    36→# subjective assessments are marked stale so reviewers know to re-evaluate.
    37→_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    38→    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    39→    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    40→    "global_mutable_config": ("initialization_coupling",),
    41→    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    42→    "layer_violation": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    43→    "private_imports": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    44→    "dupes": ("convention_outlier",),
    45→    "boilerplate_duplication": ("convention_outlier",),
    46→    "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
    47→    "flat_dirs": ("package_organization",),
    48→    "orphaned": ("design_coherence",),
    49→    "uncalled_functions": ("design_coherence",),
    50→    "responsibility_cohesion": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    51→    "cycles": ("cross_module_architecture", "dependency_health"),
    52→}
    53→
    54→
    55→def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
    56→    state: StateModel,
    57→    *,
    58→    changed_detectors: set[str],
    59→    now: str,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings change.
    62→
    63→    Only marks dimensions that already have an assessment — doesn't create
    64→    new entries for dimensions that have never been reviewed.
    65→    """
    66→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    67→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    affected_dims: set[str] = set()
    71→    for detector in changed_detectors:
    72→        dims = _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS.get(detector, ())
    73→        affected_dims.update(dims)
    74→
    75→    if not affected_dims:
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    for dimension in sorted(affected_dims):
    79→        if dimension not in assessments:
    80→            continue
    81→        payload = assessments[dimension]
    82→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    83→            continue
    84→        # Don't overwrite if already stale
    85→        if payload.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    86→            continue
    87→        payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    88→        payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_findings_changed"
    89→        payload["stale_since"] = now
    90→
    91→
    92→@dataclass
    93→class MergeScanOptions:
    94→    """Configuration bundle for merging a scan into persisted state."""
    95→
    96→    lang: str | None = None
    97→    scan_path: str | None = None
    98→    force_resolve: bool = False
    99→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   100→    potentials: dict[str, int] | None = None
   101→    merge_potentials: bool = False
   102→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   103→    include_slow: bool = True
   104→    ignore: list[str] | None = None
   105→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None
   106→
   107→
   108→def merge_scan(
   109→    state: StateModel,
   110→    current_findings: list[dict],
   111→    options: MergeScanOptions | None = None,
   112→) -> ScanDiff:
   113→    """Merge a fresh scan into existing state and return a diff summary."""
   114→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   115→    resolved_options = options or MergeScanOptions()
   116→
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    _record_scan_metadata(
   119→        state,
   120→        now,
   121→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   122→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   123→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   124→    )
   125→    _merge_scan_inputs(
   126→        state,
   127→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   128→        potentials=resolved_options.potentials,
   129→        merge_potentials=resolved_options.merge_potentials,
   130→        codebase_metrics=resolved_options.codebase_metrics,
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    existing = state["findings"]
   134→    ignore_patterns = (
   135→        resolved_options.ignore
   136→        if resolved_options.ignore is not None
   137→        else state.get("config", {}).get("ignore", [])
   138→    )
   139→    current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, current_by_detector, ignored_count, upsert_changed = (
   140→        upsert_findings(
   141→            existing,
   142→            current_findings,
   143→            ignore_patterns,
   144→            now,
   145→            lang=resolved_options.lang,
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    raw_findings = len(current_findings)
   150→    suppressed_pct = _compute_suppression(raw_findings, ignored_count)
   151→
   152→    ran_detectors = (
   153→        set(resolved_options.potentials.keys())
   154→        if resolved_options.potentials is not None
   155→        else None
   156→    )
   157→    suspect_detectors = find_suspect_detectors(
   158→        existing,
   159→        current_by_detector,
   160→        resolved_options.force_resolve,
   161→        ran_detectors,
   162→    )
   163→    auto_resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolve_changed = auto_resolve_disappeared(
   164→        existing,
   165→        current_ids,
   166→        suspect_detectors,
   167→        now,
   168→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   169→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   170→        exclude=resolved_options.exclude,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings changed.
   174→    changed_detectors = upsert_changed | resolve_changed

   177→            state, changed_detectors=changed_detectors, now=now,
   178→        )
   179→
   180→    _recompute_stats(

   183→        subjective_integrity_target=resolved_options.subjective_integrity_target,
   184→    )
   185→    _append_scan_history(

   188→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   189→        new_count=new_count,
   190→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   191→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   192→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   193→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   194→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    chronic_reopeners = [
   198→        finding
   199→        for finding in existing.values()
   200→        if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2 and finding["status"] == "open"
```

```
    20→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    21→class ReviewImportEnvelope:
    22→    """Validated shared payload shape for review imports."""
    23→
    24→    findings: list[ReviewFindingPayload]

    29→def _review_file_cache(state: dict[str, Any]) -> dict:
    30→    """Access ``state["review_cache"]["files"]``, creating if absent."""
    31→    return state.setdefault("review_cache", {}).setdefault("files", {})
    32→
    33→
    34→def _lang_potentials(state: dict[str, Any], lang_name: str) -> dict:
    35→    """Access ``state["potentials"][lang_name]``, creating if absent."""
    36→    return state.setdefault("potentials", {}).setdefault(lang_name, {})
    37→
    38→
    39→def store_assessments(
    40→    state: dict[str, Any],
    41→    assessments: dict[str, Any],
    42→    source: str,
    43→    *,
    44→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
    45→) -> None:
    46→    """Store dimension assessments in state.
    47→
    48→    *assessments*: ``{dim_name: score}`` or ``{dim_name: {score, ...}}``.
    49→    *source*: ``"per_file"`` or ``"holistic"``.
    50→
    51→    Holistic assessments overwrite per-file for the same dimension.
    52→    Per-file assessments don't overwrite holistic.
    53→    """
    54→    store = state.setdefault("subjective_assessments", {})
    55→    now = utc_now_fn()
    56→
    57→    for dimension_name, value in assessments.items():
    58→        value_obj = value if isinstance(value, dict) else {}
    59→        score = value if isinstance(value, int | float) else value_obj.get("score", 0)
    60→        score = max(0, min(100, score))
    61→        [REDACTED](str(dimension_name))
    62→        if not dimension_key:
    63→            continue
    64→
    65→        existing = store.get(dimension_key)
    66→        if existing and existing.get("source") == "holistic" and source == "per_file":
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        cleaned_components: list[str] = []
    70→        components = value_obj.get("components")
    71→        if isinstance(components, list):
    72→            cleaned_components = [
    73→                str(item).strip()
    74→                for item in components
    75→                if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
    76→            ]
    77→
    78→        component_scores = value_obj.get("component_scores")
    79→        cleaned_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    80→        if isinstance(component_scores, dict):
    81→            for key, raw in component_scores.items():
    82→                if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
    83→                    continue
    84→                if not is_numeric(raw):
    85→                    continue
    86→                cleaned_scores[key.strip()] = round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    87→
    88→        store[dimension_key] = {
    89→            "score": score,
    90→            "source": source,
    91→            "assessed_at": now,
    92→            **({"components": cleaned_components} if cleaned_components else {}),
    93→            **({"component_scores": cleaned_scores} if cleaned_scores else {}),
    94→        }
    95→
    96→
    97→def extract_reviewed_files(data: list[dict] | dict) -> list[str]:
    98→    """Parse optional reviewed-file list from import payload."""
    99→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   100→        return []
   101→    raw = data.get("reviewed_files")
   102→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
   103→        return []
   104→
   105→    reviewed: list[str] = []
   106→    seen: set[str] = set()
   107→    for item in raw:
   108→        if not isinstance(item, str):
   109→            continue
   110→        path = item.strip()
   111→        if not path or path in seen:
   112→            continue
   113→        seen.add(path)
   114→        reviewed.append(path)
   115→    return reviewed
   116→
   117→
   118→def parse_review_import_payload(
   119→    data: ReviewImportPayload | dict[str, Any],
   120→    *,
   121→    mode_name: str,
   122→) -> ReviewImportEnvelope:
   123→    """Parse shared review import payload shape for per-file/holistic flows."""
   124→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   125→        raise ValueError(f"{mode_name} review import payload must be a JSON object")
   126→
   127→    findings = data.get("findings", [])
   128→    if not isinstance(findings, list):
   129→        raise ValueError(f"{mode_name} review import payload 'findings' must be a list")
   130→    for idx, finding in enumerate(findings):
   131→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   132→            raise ValueError(
   133→                f"{mode_name} review import payload 'findings[{idx}]' must be an object"
   134→            )
   135→
   136→    assessments = data.get("assessments")
   137→    if assessments is not None and not isinstance(assessments, dict):
   138→        raise ValueError(
   139→            f"{mode_name} review import payload 'assessments' must be an object"
   140→        )
   141→    return ReviewImportEnvelope(
   142→        findings=findings,
   143→        assessments=assessments,
   144→        reviewed_files=extract_reviewed_files(data),
   145→    )
   146→
   147→
   148→def normalize_review_confidence(value: object) -> str:
   149→    """Normalize review confidence labels to high/medium/low."""
   150→    confidence = str(value).strip().lower()
   151→    return confidence if confidence in {"high", "medium", "low"} else "low"
   152→
   153→
   154→def review_tier(confidence: str, *, holistic: bool) -> int:
   155→    """Derive natural tier from review confidence and scope."""
   156→    if confidence == "high":
   157→        return 1 if holistic else 3
   158→    if confidence == "medium":
   159→        return 2 if holistic else 3
   160→    return 3
   161→
   162→
   163→def resolve_import_project_root(project_root: Path | str | None) -> Path:
   164→    """Resolve optional import project root to an absolute path."""
   165→    if project_root is None:
   166→        return get_project_root()
   167→    return Path(project_root).resolve()
   168→
   169→
   170→def upsert_review_cache_entry(
   171→    file_cache: dict[str, Any],
   172→    file_path: str,
   173→    *,
   174→    project_root: Path,
   175→    hash_file_fn,
   176→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   177→    finding_count: int | None = None,
   178→) -> None:
   179→    """Write one normalized review-cache entry for a reviewed file."""
   180→    absolute = project_root / file_path
   181→    content_hash = hash_file_fn(str(absolute)) if absolute.exists() else ""
   182→    if finding_count is None:
   183→        previous = file_cache.get(file_path, {})
   184→        count = previous.get("finding_count", 0) if isinstance(previous, dict) else 0
   185→        finding_count = count if isinstance(count, int) else 0
   186→    file_cache[file_path] = {
   187→        "content_hash": content_hash,
   188→        "reviewed_at": utc_now_fn(),
   189→        "finding_count": max(0, int(finding_count)),
   190→    }
   191→
   192→
   193→def refresh_review_file_cache(
   194→    state: dict[str, Any],
   195→    *,
   196→    reviewed_files: list[str] | None,
   197→    findings_by_file: dict[str, int | None] | None = None,
   198→    project_root: Path | str | None = None,
   199→    hash_file_fn,
   200→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   201→) -> None:
   202→    """Refresh normalized review cache entries for all reviewed files."""
   203→    file_cache = _review_file_cache(state)
   204→    resolved_project_root = resolve_import_project_root(project_root)
   205→    counts = findings_by_file or {}
   206→
   207→    reviewed_set = set(counts)
   208→    if reviewed_files:
   209→        reviewed_set.update(
   210→            str(file_path).strip()
   211→            for file_path in reviewed_files
   212→            if isinstance(file_path, str) and str(file_path).strip()
   213→        )
   214→
   215→    for file_path in reviewed_set:
   216→        upsert_review_cache_entry(
   217→            file_cache,
   218→            file_path,
   219→            project_root=resolved_project_root,
   220→            hash_file_fn=hash_file_fn,
   221→            utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   222→            finding_count=counts.get(file_path),
   223→        )
   224→
   225→
   226→def auto_resolve_review_findings(
   227→    state: dict[str, Any],
   228→    *,
   229→    new_ids: set[str],
   230→    diff: dict[str, Any],
   231→    note: str,
   232→    should_resolve: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], bool],
   233→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   234→) -> None:
   235→    """Auto-resolve stale open review findings that match a scope predicate."""
   236→    diff.setdefault("auto_resolved", 0)
   237→    for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
   238→        if finding_id in new_ids or finding.get("status") != "open":
   239→            continue
   240→        if not should_resolve(finding):
   241→            continue
   242→        finding["status"] = "auto_resolved"
   243→        finding["resolved_at"] = utc_now_fn()
   244→        finding["note"] = note
   245→        diff["auto_resolved"] += 1
   246→
```

```
1→"""State-application helpers for resolve command flows."""

     3→from __future__ import annotations

     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query_best_effort as _write_query_best_effort
     9→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    10→
    11→from .selection import ResolveQueryContext
    12→
    13→
    14→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    15→    state: dict,
    16→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    17→    *,
    18→    attestation: str | None,
    19→) -> list[str]:
    20→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    21→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    22→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    23→            state,
    24→            pattern,
    25→            args.status,
    26→            args.note,
    27→            attestation=attestation,
    28→        )
    29→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    30→    return all_resolved
    31→
    32→
    33→def write_query(payload: dict) -> OutputResult:
    34→    """Backward-compatible resolve query writer seam."""
    35→    return _write_query_best_effort(
    36→        payload,
    37→        context="resolve query payload update",
    38→    )
    39→
    40→
    41→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    42→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)
    43→    write_query(
    44→        {
    45→            "command": "resolve",
    46→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    47→            "status": context.status,
    48→            "resolved": context.resolved,
    49→            "count": len(context.resolved),
    50→            "next_command": context.next_command,
    51→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    52→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    53→            "strict_score": scores.strict,

    55→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,

    57→            "prev_strict_score": context.prev_strict,
    58→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    59→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    60→            "narrative": context.narrative,
    61→        }
    62→    )
    63→
```

```
   272→def _update_objective_health(
   273→    state: StateModel,
   274→    findings: dict,
   275→    *,
   276→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   277→) -> None:
   278→    """Compute canonical score trio from dimension scoring."""
   279→    pots = state.get("potentials", {})
   280→    if not pots:
   281→        return
   282→
   283→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with desloppify.scoring
   284→    # (scoring -> _scoring/results/core -> _scoring/subjective/core -> review -> state -> _state/merge -> _state/scoring)
   285→    from desloppify.scoring import (
   286→        compute_health_score,
   287→        compute_score_bundle,
   288→        merge_potentials,
   289→    )
   290→
   291→    merged = merge_potentials(pots)
   292→    if not merged:
   293→        return
   294→
   295→    subjective_assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or None
   296→    integrity_target = (
   297→        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(subjective_integrity_target)))
   298→        if isinstance(subjective_integrity_target, int | float)
   299→        else None
   300→    )
   301→    integrity_meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(integrity_target)
   302→    if subjective_assessments and integrity_target is not None:
   303→        subjective_assessments, integrity_meta = _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
   304→            subjective_assessments,
   305→            target=integrity_target,
   306→        )
   307→    state["subjective_integrity"] = integrity_meta
   308→
   309→    has_active_checks = any((count or 0) > 0 for count in merged.values())
   310→    if not has_active_checks and not subjective_assessments:
   311→        state["dimension_scores"] = {}
   312→        state["overall_score"] = 100.0
   313→        state["objective_score"] = 100.0
   314→        state["strict_score"] = 100.0
   315→        state["verified_strict_score"] = 100.0
   316→        return
   317→
   318→    # Derive language-scoped subjective dimensions so only dimensions with
   319→    # a review pathway get 0-score placeholders.  Explicit assessments
   320→    # outside this set still count (append_subjective_dimensions handles
   321→    # that).  Without this, all 20 global default dimensions would get
   322→    # placeholders — including ones the language's review never covers.
   323→    allowed_subjective: set[str] | None = None
   324→    lang_name = _resolve_lang_from_state(state)
   325→    if lang_name:
   326→        try:
   327→            from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.lang import (
   328→                HOLISTIC_DIMENSIONS_BY_LANG,
   329→            )
   330→
   331→            lang_dims = HOLISTIC_DIMENSIONS_BY_LANG.get(lang_name)
   332→            if lang_dims:
   333→                allowed_subjective = set(lang_dims)
   334→        except (ImportError, AttributeError):
   335→            pass
   336→
   337→    bundle = compute_score_bundle(
   338→        findings,
   339→        merged,
   340→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   341→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective,
   342→    )
   343→    lenient_scores = bundle.dimension_scores
   344→    strict_scores = bundle.strict_dimension_scores
   345→    verified_strict_scores = bundle.verified_strict_dimension_scores
   346→
   347→    prev_dim_scores = dict(state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   348→
   349→    state["dimension_scores"] = {
   350→        name: dict(
   351→            score=lenient_scores[name]["score"],
   352→            strict_score=strict_scores[name]["score"],
   353→            verified_strict_score=verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
   354→            checks=lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
   355→            issues=lenient_scores[name]["issues"],
   356→            tier=lenient_scores[name]["tier"],
   357→            detectors=lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
   358→        )
   359→        for name in lenient_scores

   361→    for data in state["dimension_scores"].values():
   362→        data["strict"] = data["strict_score"]
   363→
   364→    # Carry forward mechanical dimensions from a prior scan that are absent
   365→    # now (e.g. duplication when --skip-slow is used).
   366→    for dim_name, prev_data in prev_dim_scores.items():
   367→        if dim_name in state["dimension_scores"]:

   370→            continue
   371→        if "subjective_assessment" in prev_data.get("detectors", {}):
   372→            continue
   373→        carried = {**prev_data, "carried_forward": True}
   374→        # Backfill for state files written before verified_strict_score existed.
   375→        carried.setdefault(
   376→            "verified_strict_score",
   377→            carried.get("strict_score", carried.get("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0))),
   378→        )
   379→        state["dimension_scores"][dim_name] = carried
   380→
   381→    _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   382→        state,
   383→        dimension_scores=state["dimension_scores"],
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    state.update(_aggregate_scores(state["dimension_scores"], compute_health_score))
   387→
   388→
   389→def _recompute_stats(
   390→    state: StateModel,
   391→    scan_path: str | None = None,
   392→    *,
   393→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   394→) -> None:
   395→    """Recompute stats and canonical health scores from findings."""
   396→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   397→    findings = path_scoped_findings(state["findings"], scan_path)
   398→    counters, tier_stats = _count_findings(findings)
   399→    state["stats"] = {
   400→        "total": sum(counters.values()),
   401→        **counters,
   402→        "by_tier": {
   403→            str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
   404→        },
   405→    }
   406→    _update_objective_health(
   407→        state,
   408→        findings,
   409→        subjective_integrity_target=subjective_integrity_target,
   410→    )
   411→
   412→
   413→def _empty_suppression_metrics() -> dict[str, int | float]:
   414→    return {
   415→        "last_ignored": 0,
   416→        "last_raw_findings": 0,
   417→        "last_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   418→        "last_ignore_patterns": 0,
   419→        "recent_scans": 0,
   420→        "recent_ignored": 0,
   421→        "recent_raw_findings": 0,
   422→        "recent_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   423→    }
   424→
   425→
   426→def suppression_metrics(state: StateModel, *, window: int = 5) -> dict[str, int | float]:
   427→    """Summarize ignore suppression from recent scan history."""
   428→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   429→    if not history:
   430→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   431→
   432→    scans_with_suppression = [
   433→        entry
   434→        for entry in history
   435→        if isinstance(entry, dict)
   436→        and (
   437→            "ignored" in entry
   438→            or "raw_findings" in entry
   439→            or "suppressed_pct" in entry
   440→            or "ignore_patterns" in entry
   441→        )
   442→    ]
   443→    if not scans_with_suppression:
   444→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   445→
   446→    recent = scans_with_suppression[-max(1, window) :]
   447→    last = recent[-1]
   448→
   449→    recent_ignored = sum(int(entry.get("ignored", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
```

```
   101→def _build_dimensions() -> list[Dimension]:
   102→    """Derive dimensions from DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.
   103→
   104→    Each unique (dimension, tier) pair becomes a Dimension, with its detectors
   105→    collected automatically. Order follows first-seen in the policies dict.
   106→    """
   107→    # Collect (dimension_name -> tier) preserving first-seen order,
   108→    # and group detectors by dimension.
   109→    dim_tiers: dict[str, int] = {}
   110→    grouped: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   111→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items():
   112→        if policy.dimension is None or policy.tier is None:
   113→            continue
   114→        if policy.dimension not in dim_tiers:
   115→            dim_tiers[policy.dimension] = policy.tier
   116→            grouped[policy.dimension] = []
   117→        grouped[policy.dimension].append(detector)
   118→    return [
   119→        Dimension(name=name, tier=tier, detectors=grouped[name])
   120→        for name, tier in dim_tiers.items()
   121→    ]
   122→
   123→
   124→DIMENSIONS = _build_dimensions()
   125→DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME = {d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS}
   126→
   127→TIER_WEIGHTS = {
   128→    Tier.AUTO_FIX: 1,
   129→    Tier.QUICK_FIX: 2,
   130→    Tier.JUDGMENT: 3,
   131→    Tier.MAJOR_REFACTOR: 4,
   132→}
   133→CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS = {Confidence.HIGH: 1.0, Confidence.MEDIUM: 0.7, Confidence.LOW: 0.3}
   134→
   135→# Minimum checks for full dimension weight — below this, weight is dampened
   136→# proportionally. Prevents small-sample dimensions from swinging the overall score.
   137→MIN_SAMPLE = 200
   138→
   139→# Holistic review weight: findings with file="." and detail.holistic=True
   140→# get a 10x weight multiplier for display/priority purposes (issues list,
   141→# remediation engine).  NOT used in score computation — review findings are
   142→# excluded from the detection scoring pipeline (scored via assessments only).
   143→HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER = 10.0
   144→HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL = 10
   145→
   146→# Budget: subjective dimensions get this fraction of the overall score.
   147→# Mechanical dimensions get the remainder.
   148→SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.60
   149→MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 1.0 - SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   150→
   151→# Per-dimension weighting within the mechanical pool.
   152→# Keep this balanced: no special boost for security/test in the pool itself.
   153→MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   154→    "file health": 2.0,
   155→    "code quality": 1.0,
   156→    "duplication": 1.0,
   157→    "test health": 1.0,
   158→    "security": 1.0,
   159→}
   160→
   161→# Per-dimension weighting within the subjective pool.
   162→# Emphasize elegance and contract/type coherence for a stronger architecture north star.
   163→SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   164→    "high elegance": 22.0,
   165→    "mid elegance": 22.0,
   166→    "low elegance": 12.0,
   167→    "contracts": 12.0,
   168→    "type safety": 12.0,
   169→    "abstraction fit": 8.0,
   170→    "logic clarity": 6.0,
   171→    # Low-but-meaningful structural signal (about half of the subjective
   172→    # average weight) so it matters without dominating craftsmanship axes.
   173→    "structure nav": 5.0,
   174→    "error consistency": 3.0,
   175→    "naming quality": 2.0,
   176→    "ai generated debt": 1.0,
   177→    "design coherence": 10.0,
   178→}
   179→
   180→# Synthetic check count for subjective dimensions in dimension_scores.
   181→SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS = 10
   182→
   183→FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE: dict[ScoreMode, frozenset[str]] = {
   184→    "lenient": frozenset({"open"}),
   185→    "strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix"}),
   186→    "verified_strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "fixed", "false_positive"}),
   187→}
   188→
   189→# Tolerance for treating a subjective score as "on target" in integrity checks.
   190→# Scores within this band of the target are flagged as potential gaming.
   191→SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE = 0.05
   192→
   193→
   194→def matches_target_score(
   195→    score: object,
   196→    target: object,
   197→    *,
   198→    tolerance: float = SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
   199→) -> bool:
   200→    """Return True when score is within tolerance of target."""
   201→    try:
   202→        score_value = float(score)
   203→        target_value = float(target)
   204→        tolerance_value = max(0.0, float(tolerance))
   205→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   206→        return False
   207→    return abs(score_value - target_value) <= tolerance_value
   208→
   209→
```

```
    15→def _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, history: list[dict], state: StateModel) -> dict:

    17→    if not dim_scores:

    21→    return {
    22→        "lowest_dimensions": _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores, potentials),
    23→        "biggest_gap_dimensions": _biggest_gap_dimensions(dim_scores, state)[:3],

    27→
    28→def _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, potentials: dict) -> list[dict]:
    29→    """Build summary entries for the lowest strict-scoring dimensions."""
    30→    sorted_dims = sorted(
    31→        (
    32→            (name, ds)
    33→            for name, ds in dim_scores.items()
    34→            if ds.get("strict", ds["score"]) < 100
    35→        ),
    36→        key=lambda item: item[1].get("strict", item[1]["score"]),
```
