    33→
    34→def _display_fallback(dim_name: str) -> str:
    35→    words = dim_name.replace("_", " ")
    36→    return words[0].upper() + words[1:] if words else words
    37→
    38→
    39→def _normalize_dimension_key(dim_name: object) -> str:
    40→    if not isinstance(dim_name, str):
    41→        return ""
    42→    return "_".join(dim_name.strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())
    43→
    44→
    45→def _primary_lang_from_findings(findings: dict) -> str | None:
    46→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    47→    for finding in findings.values():
    48→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
    49→            continue
    50→        raw_lang = finding.get("lang")
    51→        if not isinstance(raw_lang, str) or not raw_lang.strip():
    52→            continue
    53→        key = raw_lang.strip().lower()
    54→        counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    55→    if not counts:
    56→        return None
    57→    return max(counts, key=counts.get)
    58→
    59→
    60→def _dimension_display_name(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> str:
    61→    try:
    62→        # Deferred import to avoid circular: scoring -> _scoring/results/core
    63→        # -> _scoring/subjective/core -> intelligence.review -> state -> scoring
    64→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    65→            dimension_display_name,
    66→        )
    67→
    68→        return str(dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    69→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    70→        return DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim_name, _display_fallback(dim_name))
    71→
    72→
    73→def _dimension_weight(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> float:
    74→    try:
    75→        # Deferred import to avoid circular (see _dimension_display_name).
    76→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    77→            dimension_weight,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→        return float(dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    81→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    82→        return 1.0
    83→
    84→
    85→def append_subjective_dimensions(
    86→    results: dict,
    87→    findings: dict,
    88→    assessments: dict | None,
    89→    failure_set: frozenset[str],
    90→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    91→) -> None:
    92→    """Append subjective review dimensions to results dict (mutates results).
    93→
    94→    Subjective scoring is evidence-first: open review findings for a dimension
    95→    determine pass-rate, while imported assessment scores are retained as
    96→    metadata for transparency.
    97→    """
    98→    # Deferred import to avoid circular (see _dimension_display_name).
    99→    from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.holistic import DIMENSIONS
   100→
   101→    raw_defaults = DIMENSIONS
   102→    allowed = (
   103→        {_normalize_dimension_key(name) for name in allowed_dimensions}
   104→        if allowed_dimensions is not None
   105→        else None
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    default_dimensions = []
   109→    for raw_dim in raw_defaults:
   110→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
   111→        if not dim:
   112→            continue
   113→        if allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
   114→            continue
   115→        default_dimensions.append(dim)
   116→
   117→    assessed: dict[str, dict] = {}
   118→    for raw_dim, payload in (assessments or {}).items():
   119→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
   120→        if not dim:
   121→            continue
   122→        # Real assessments (from review imports) always count regardless of
   123→        # allowed_dimensions — they were deliberately imported and should not
   124→        # be silently discarded.  However, system-generated placeholders
   125→        # (scan_reset_subjective) are not real assessments and should respect
   126→        # the allowed_dimensions scope like defaults.
   127→        is_placeholder = isinstance(payload, dict) and (
   128→            payload.get("placeholder") is True
   129→            or payload.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   130→            or payload.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   131→        )
   132→        if is_placeholder and allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
   133→            continue
   134→        assessed[dim] = payload
   135→    existing_lower = {k.lower() for k in results}
   136→    lang_name = _primary_lang_from_findings(findings)
   137→
   138→    all_dims = list(default_dimensions)
   139→    for dim_name in assessed:
   140→        if dim_name not in default_dimensions:
   141→            all_dims.append(dim_name)
   142→
   143→    for dim_name in all_dims:
   144→        is_default = dim_name in default_dimensions
   145→        assessment = assessed.get(dim_name)
   146→        has_assessment = isinstance(assessment, dict)
   147→        if not is_default and not assessment:
   148→            continue
   149→
   150→        display = _dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   151→        if display.lower() in existing_lower:
   152→            display = f"{display} (subjective)"
   153→
   154→        # Count open review/concern findings for display (work queue), but
   155→        # these do NOT drive the dimension score — only assessment scores do.
   156→        issue_count = sum(
   157→            1
   158→            for finding in findings.values()
   159→            if finding.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   160→            and finding.get("status") in failure_set
   161→            and _normalize_dimension_key(
   162→                finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension")
   163→            )
   164→            == dim_name
   165→        )
   166→
   167→        assessment_score = (
   168→            max(0.0, min(100.0, float(assessment.get("score", 0))))
   169→            if isinstance(assessment, dict)
   170→            else 0.0
   171→        )
   172→        integrity_penalty = (
   173→            assessment.get("integrity_penalty")
   174→            if isinstance(assessment, dict)
   175→            else None
   176→        )
   177→        reset_pending = bool(
   178→            isinstance(assessment, dict)
   179→            and (
   180→                assessment.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   181→                or assessment.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   182→                or assessment.get("placeholder") is True
   183→            )
   184→        )
   185→        if reset_pending:
   186→            score = 0.0
   187→            pass_rate = 0.0
   188→        elif integrity_penalty == "target_match_reset":
   189→            score = 0.0
   190→            pass_rate = 0.0
   191→        elif has_assessment:
   192→            # Assessment score drives the dimension score directly.
   193→            # Resolving review findings does NOT change this score —
   194→            # only a fresh review import updates it.
   195→            score = assessment_score
   196→            pass_rate = score / 100.0
   197→        else:
   198→            # No assessment yet: explicit unassessed placeholder.
   199→            # Subjective dimensions start at 0 until an assessment is imported.
   200→            score = 0.0
   201→            pass_rate = 0.0
   202→        components: list[str] = []
   203→        component_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   204→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
   205→            raw_components = assessment.get("components")
   206→            if isinstance(raw_components, list):
   207→                components = [
   208→                    str(item).strip()
   209→                    for item in raw_components
   210→                    if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
   211→                ]
   212→            raw_component_scores = assessment.get("component_scores")
   213→            if isinstance(raw_component_scores, dict):
   214→                for key, value in raw_component_scores.items():
   215→                    if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
   216→                        continue
   217→                    if not is_numeric(value):
   218→                        continue
   219→                    component_scores[key.strip()] = round(
   220→                        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))),
   221→                        1,
   222→                    )
   223→
   224→        results[display] = {
   225→            "score": round(float(score), 1),
   226→            "tier": 4,
   227→            "checks": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   228→            "issues": issue_count,
   229→            "detectors": {
   230→                "subjective_assessment": {
   231→                    "potential": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   232→                    "pass_rate": round(pass_rate, 4),
   233→                    "issues": issue_count,
   234→                    "weighted_failures": round(SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS * (1 - pass_rate), 4),
   235→                    "assessment_score": round(assessment_score, 1),
   236→                    "placeholder": reset_pending or not has_assessment,
   237→                    "dimension_key": dim_name,
   238→                    "configured_weight": round(
   239→                        _dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name), 6
   240→                    ),
   241→                    "components": components,
   242→                    **(
   243→                        {"component_scores": component_scores}
   244→                        if component_scores
   245→                        else {}
   246→                    ),
   247→                }
   248→            },
   249→        }
   250→
   251→
   252→__all__ = [
   253→    "DISPLAY_NAMES",
   254→    "append_subjective_dimensions",
   255→]

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "suppression_metrics",

    10→
    11→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import coerce_confidence
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import matches_target_score
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_findings
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, ensure_state_defaults
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import ScanCoverageRecord
    16→
    17→_EMPTY_COUNTERS = ("open", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "wontfix", "false_positive")
    18→_SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD = 2
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    22→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    23→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    24→    if isinstance(history, list):
    25→        for entry in reversed(history):
    26→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    28→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    29→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    30→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    31→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    32→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    33→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    34→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    35→    return None
    36→
    37→
    38→def _count_findings(findings: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    39→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    40→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    41→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    42→
    43→    for finding in findings.values():
    44→        status = finding["status"]
    45→        tier = finding.get("tier", 3)
    46→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    47→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    48→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    49→
    50→    return counters, tier_stats
    51→
    52→
    53→def _coerce_subjective_score(value: dict | float | int | str | None) -> float:
    54→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float."""
    55→    raw = value.get("score", 0) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
    56→    try:
    57→        score = float(raw)
    58→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    59→        score = 0.0
    60→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, score))
    61→
    62→
    63→def _subjective_target_matches(
    64→    subjective_assessments: dict, *, target: float
    65→) -> list[str]:
    66→    """Return dimension keys whose subjective score matches the target band."""
    67→    matches = [
    68→        dimension
    69→        for dimension, payload in subjective_assessments.items()
    70→        if matches_target_score(_coerce_subjective_score(payload), target)
    71→    ]
    72→    return sorted(matches)
    73→
    74→
    75→def _subjective_integrity_baseline(target: float | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    76→    """Create baseline subjective-integrity metadata for scan/reporting output."""
    77→    return {
    78→        "status": "disabled" if target is None else "pass",
    79→        "target_score": None if target is None else round(float(target), 2),
    80→        "matched_count": 0,

    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,

    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats

    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→

    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    84→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    85→        return (
    86→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
    87→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
    88→            subjective_score_value(item),
    89→            item.get("id", ""),
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    detail = detail_dict(item)
    93→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
    94→    return (
    95→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
    96→        0,
    97→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
    98→        -review_weight,
    99→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   100→        item.get("id", ""),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   105→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   106→
   107→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   108→        return {
   109→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   110→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   111→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   112→            "policy": (
   113→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   114→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   115→            ),
   116→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   117→        }
   118→
   119→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   120→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   121→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   122→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   123→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   124→    if is_subjective:
   125→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   126→    elif is_review:
   127→        ranking_factors = [
   128→            "tier asc",
   129→            "confidence asc",
   130→            "review_weight desc",
   131→            "count desc",
   132→            "id asc",
   133→        ]
   134→    else:
   135→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   136→    explain = {
   137→        "kind": "finding",
   138→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   139→        "confidence": confidence,
   140→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   141→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   142→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   143→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   144→    }
   145→    if is_review:
   146→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→    if is_subjective:
   148→        explain["policy"] = (
   149→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   150→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   151→        )
   152→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   153→    return explain
   154→
   155→
   156→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   157→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   158→    for item in items:
   159→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   160→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   161→    return counts
   162→
   163→
   164→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   165→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   166→    if not available:
   167→        return None
   168→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   169→
   170→
   171→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   172→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   173→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   174→    for item in items:
   175→        if group == "file":
   176→            key = item.get("file", "")
   177→        elif group == "detector":
   178→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   179→        elif group == "tier":
   180→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   181→        elif group == "cluster":
   182→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   183→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   184→        else:
   185→            key = "items"
   186→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   187→    return grouped
   188→
   189→
   190→__all__ = [
   191→    "build_finding_items",
   192→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   193→    "item_explain",
   194→    "item_sort_key",
   195→    "tier_counts",
   196→    "subjective_score_value",
   197→    "group_queue_items",
   198→]
   199→

    35→def show_detector_progress(state: dict):
    36→    """Show per-detector progress bars — the heartbeat of a scan."""
    37→    return presentation_mod.show_detector_progress(
    38→        state,
    39→        state_mod=state_mod,
    40→        narrative_mod=narrative_mod,
    41→        registry_mod=registry_mod,

    43→    )

    45→
    46→def _dimension_bar(score: float, *, bar_len: int = 15) -> str:
    47→    """Render a score bar consistent with scan detector bars."""
    48→    return presentation_mod.dimension_bar(score, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
    49→
    50→
    51→def scorecard_dimension_entries(
    52→    state: dict,
    53→    *,
    54→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    55→) -> list[dict]:
    56→    """Return scorecard rows with presentation-friendly metadata."""
    57→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    58→    subjective_by_name = {
    59→        entry["name"]: entry
    60→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(
    61→            state,
    62→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
    63→        )
    64→    }
    65→    entries: list[dict] = []
    66→    for name, data in rows:
    67→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    68→        subjective_meta = subjective_by_name.get(name)
    69→        is_subjective = subjective_meta is not None
    70→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    71→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    72→        issues = int(data.get("issues", 0))
    73→        checks = int(data.get("checks", 0))
    74→        placeholder = bool(subjective_meta.get("placeholder")) if subjective_meta else False
    75→        not_scanned = bool(
    76→            not is_subjective and not detectors and checks == 0
    77→        )
    78→        carried_forward = bool(
    79→            not is_subjective and data.get("carried_forward")
    80→        )
    81→        dim_key = str(subjective_meta.get("dimension_key", "")) if subjective_meta else ""
    82→        stale = bool(subjective_meta.get("stale")) if subjective_meta else False
    83→        cli_keys = (
    84→            list(subjective_meta.get("cli_keys", []))
    85→            if subjective_meta
    86→            else scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data)
    87→        )
    88→        entries.append(
    89→            {
    90→                "name": name,
    91→                "score": score,
    92→                "strict": strict,
    93→                "issues": issues,
    94→                "checks": checks,
    95→                "subjective": is_subjective,
    96→                "placeholder": placeholder,
    97→                "stale": stale,
    98→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
    99→                "not_scanned": not_scanned,
   100→                "carried_forward": carried_forward,
   101→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   102→            }
   103→        )
   104→    return entries
   105→
   106→
   107→def show_scorecard_subjective_measures(state: dict) -> None:
   108→    """Show canonical scorecard dimensions only (mechanical + subjective)."""
   109→    entries = scorecard_dimension_entries(state)
   110→    if not entries:
   111→        return
   112→
   113→    print(colorize("  Scorecard dimensions (matches scorecard.png):", "dim"))
   114→    for entry in entries:
   115→        if entry.get("not_scanned"):
   116→            print(
   117→                "  "
   118→                + f"{entry['name']:<18} "
   119→                + colorize("─── skipped ───────────────────  (run without --skip-slow)", "yellow")
   120→            )
   121→            continue
   122→        bar = _dimension_bar(entry["score"])
   123→        suffix = ""
   124→        if entry.get("carried_forward"):
   125→            suffix = colorize("  ⟲ prior scan", "dim")
   126→        elif entry.get("placeholder"):
   127→            suffix = colorize("  [unassessed]", "yellow")
   128→        elif entry.get("stale"):
   129→            suffix = colorize("  [stale — re-review]", "yellow")

   133→            + colorize(f"(strict {entry['strict']:5.1f}%)", "dim")
   134→            + suffix
   135→        )
   136→    stale_keys = [e["dimension_key"] for e in entries if e.get("stale")]
   137→    has_open = any(
   138→        f.get("status") == "open" and not f.get("suppressed")
   139→        for f in (state.get("findings") or {}).values()
   140→    )
   141→    if stale_keys and not has_open:
   142→        n = len(stale_keys)
   143→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   144→        print(

   148→                "yellow",
   149→            )
   150→        )
   151→    print()
   152→

   155→    """Show score recipe and weighted drags so users can see what drives the north star."""

1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and finding pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "finding_in_scan_scope",
     9→    "open_scope_breakdown",
    10→    "path_scoped_findings",
    11→    "is_ignored",

    17→
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    19→    Finding,
    20→    StateModel,
    21→    ensure_state_defaults,
    22→    utc_now,
    23→    validate_state_invariants,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    26→
    27→
    28→def path_scoped_findings(
    29→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    30→    scan_path: str | None,
    31→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    32→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    33→    return {
    34→        finding_id: finding
    35→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    36→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    37→    }
    38→
    39→
    40→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    41→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    42→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    43→        return True
    44→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    45→    return (
    46→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    47→        or file_path == scan_path
    48→        or file_path == "."
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def open_scope_breakdown(
    53→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    54→    scan_path: str | None,
    55→    *,
    56→    detector: str | None = None,
    57→) -> dict[str, int]:
    58→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    59→    in_scope = 0
    60→    out_of_scope = 0
    61→
    62→    for finding in findings.values():
    63→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    64→            continue
    65→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    66→            continue
    67→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    68→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    69→            in_scope += 1
    70→        else:
    71→            out_of_scope += 1
    72→
    73→    return {
    74→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    75→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    76→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    77→    }
    78→
    79→
    80→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    81→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    82→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    83→
    84→
    85→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    86→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    87→) -> str | None:
    88→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    89→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    90→        if "*" in pattern:
    91→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    92→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    93→                return pattern
    94→            continue
    95→
    96→        if "::" in pattern:
    97→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
    98→                return pattern
    99→            continue
   100→
   101→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   102→            return pattern
   103→
   104→    return None
   105→
   106→
   107→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   108→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   109→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   110→    matched_ids = [
   111→        finding_id
   112→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   113→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   114→    ]
   115→    now = utc_now()
   116→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   117→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   118→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   119→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   120→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   121→        if finding.get("status") in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"):
   122→            finding["status"] = "open"
   123→            finding["resolved_at"] = None
   124→            finding["note"] = (
   125→                "Suppressed by ignore pattern — remains unresolved for score integrity"
   126→            )
   127→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   128→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   129→
   130→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   131→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   132→    return len(matched_ids)
   133→
   134→
   135→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   136→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   137→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   138→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   139→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   140→    if pattern not in ignores:
   141→        ignores.append(pattern)
   142→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   143→
   144→
   145→def make_finding(
   146→    detector: str,
   147→    file: str,
   148→    name: str,
   149→    *,
   150→    tier: int,
   151→    confidence: str,
   152→    summary: str,
   153→    detail: dict | None = None,
   154→) -> Finding:
   155→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   156→    rfile = rel(file)
   157→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   158→    now = utc_now()
   159→    return {
   160→        "id": finding_id,
   161→        "detector": detector,
   162→        "file": rfile,
   163→        "tier": tier,
   164→        "confidence": confidence,
   165→        "summary": summary,
   166→        "detail": detail or {},
   167→        "status": "open",
   168→        "note": None,
   169→        "first_seen": now,
   170→        "last_seen": now,
   171→        "resolved_at": None,
   172→        "reopen_count": 0,
   173→    }
   174→
   175→
   176→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   177→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, or path."""
   178→    return (
   179→        finding_id == pattern
   180→        or ("*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern))
   181→        or ("::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern))
   182→        or (
   183→            finding.get("detector") == pattern
   184→            or finding["file"] == pattern
   185→            or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   186→        )
   187→    )
   188→

## Complete Map: Scope Flow from User Input to Finding Selection

Based on thorough analysis of the codebase, here is the complete pattern-to-entity resolution architecture:

---

### **TWO DISTINCT PATTERN MATCHING SYSTEMS**

The codebase has **two separate pattern-matching pipelines** that work in parallel but have different purposes:

#### **SYSTEM 1: WORK QUEUE SCOPE MATCHING** (Show/Next/Status/Planning)
Used by display/viewing commands that need flexible pattern matching with fnmatch support.

**Entry Point:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/scope.py`
- `resolve_show_scope()` (line 107) — Parse CLI args to extract pattern/scope
- `load_matches()` (line 127) — Primary function that builds the work queue with scope filtering

**Flow for `show` command:**
```
cmd_show() [show/cmd.py:62]
  ↓
resolve_show_scope() [scope.py:107]
  Returns: (ok, pattern, status_filter, scope)
  ↓
load_matches() [scope.py:127]
  Calls: build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(scope=scope, ...))
  ↓
build_work_queue() [_work_queue/core.py:139]
  ↓
  1. build_finding_items() [ranking.py:28]
     For each finding: if not scope_matches(item, scope): continue
  ↓
  2. build_subjective_items() [helpers.py:198]
     For each subjective item: if scope_matches(item, scope): all_items.append(item)
  ↓
  Returns: WorkQueueResult with filtered items
```

**The Core Scope Matcher: `scope_matches()`**
Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py:63`

```python
def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    if not scope:
        return True
    
    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    
    # BRANCH 1: Wildcard patterns
    if "*" in scope:
        return any(
            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
        )
    
    # BRANCH 2: ID prefix patterns (::)
    if "::" in scope:
        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    
    # BRANCH 3: Subjective dimension matching
    lowered = scope.lower()
    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
        return (
            lowered in item_id.lower()
            or lowered in dimension.lower()
            or lowered in summary.lower()
        )
    
    # BRANCH 4: Hash suffix matching (8+ hex chars)
    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    
    # BRANCH 5: Direct matching (detector, file exact, or file prefix)
    return (
        detector == scope
        or filepath == scope
        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
    )
```

**Lines 63-100** — Five distinct matching branches

**Callers of `scope_matches()`:**
1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py:76` — In `build_finding_items()`, filter mechanical findings
2. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py:176` — In `build_work_queue()`, filter subjective items

#### **SYSTEM 2: STATE RESOLUTION PATTERN MATCHING** (Resolve/Ignore)
Used by state-mutating commands that need to match findings for status changes.

**Entry Point:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/filtering.py:176`

```
cmd_resolve() [resolve/cmd.py:57]
  ↓
_resolve_all_patterns() [resolve/apply.py:14]
  For each pattern:
    ↓
    state_mod.resolve_findings(state, pattern, ...) 
      [engine/_state/resolution.py:97]
      ↓
      match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter)
        [engine/_state/resolution.py:83]
        ↓
        For each finding:
          if _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern): include
        [engine/_state/filtering.py:176]
```

**The Core Pattern Matcher: `_matches_pattern()`**
Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/filtering.py:176`

```python
def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, or path."""
    return (
        finding_id == pattern                                    # Exact ID match
        or ("*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern))  # Glob on ID
        or ("::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern))  # ID prefix
        or (
            finding.get("detector") == pattern
            or finding["file"] == pattern
            or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
        )
    )
```

**Lines 176-187** — Similar but **simpler than `scope_matches()`**:
- No wildcard matching on summary/dimension
- No hash suffix matching
- No subjective dimension special handling
- Only matches against ID and detector/file

---

### **DIMENSION NAME → DETECTOR REWRITING** (Show-Only Feature)

**Function:** `try_dimension_rewrite()` and `load_matches_for_dimension()`
Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/scope.py`

**Purpose:** Allow users to type dimension names (e.g., "file_health") and see findings from all detectors in that dimension.

```
show file_health
  ↓
resolve_show_scope(args) [scope.py:107]
  Returns: scope = "file_health"
  ↓
load_matches(state, scope="file_health", ...)
  Calls build_work_queue with scope="file_health"
  ↓
build_finding_items() runs scope_matches(..., "file_health")
  No items match (file_health is not a detector/file/ID)
  ↓
Back in cmd_show(): if not matches:
  ↓
load_matches_for_dimension(state, "file_health", status_filter)
  [scope.py:59]
  ↓
_build_dimension_lookup() [scope.py:14]
  Builds: {"file_health": ["detector1", "detector2", ...]}
  ↓
For each detector in dimension:
  load_matches(state, scope=detector, ...)
  ↓
All findings aggregated and deduplicated by ID
```

**Key Functions:**
- `_build_dimension_lookup()` [line 14] — Creates map of dimension names/keys → detector lists
- `try_dimension_rewrite()` [line 40] — Single-detector case (returns detector name)
- `load_matches_for_dimension()` [line 59] — Multi-detector case (aggregates)

**Note:** `try_dimension_rewrite()` is defined but **only called internally within scope.py** — not actually invoked by the show command (would fall through to `load_matches_for_dimension` fallback).

---

### **ALL BUILD_WORK_QUEUE CALLERS**

The work queue is the unified entry point for scope filtering:

1. **`show` command** [show/cmd.py:91]
   ```python
   matches = load_matches(state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic)
   ```
   Internally calls `build_work_queue()`

2. **`next` command** [next.py:112]
   ```python
   queue = build_work_queue(
       state,
       options=QueueBuildOptions(
           tier=tier, count=count, scope=scope, status=status,
           include_subjective=True, plan=plan_data, ...
       ),
   )
   ```

3. **`show` dimension fallback** [show/cmd.py:101]
   ```python
   dim_matches, dim_display = load_matches_for_dimension(
       state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter
   )
   ```
   Calls `load_matches()` → `build_work_queue()` for each detector

4. **`planning/select.py`** [planning/select.py:23]
   ```python
   result = build_work_queue(
       state,
       options=QueueBuildOptions(
           tier=tier, count=count, scan_path=scan_path,
           status="open", include_subjective=False, no_tier_fallback=True
       ),
   )
   ```
   Legacy planner API (note: `scope=None` — no pattern filtering)

5. **`planning/render.py`** [planning/render.py:190, 450]
   ```python
   queue = build_work_queue(queue_state, options=QueueBuildOptions(...))
   ```
   Two calls for scorecard and full queue lookup

6. **`review/preflight.py`** [review/preflight.py:83]
   ```python
   result = build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(...))
   ```
   Review blocking dimensions check

### **QUEUEBUILDOPTIONS SIGNATURE**

Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py:25`

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueBuildOptions:
    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    
    tier: int | None = None
    count: int | None = 1
    scan_path: str | None = None
    scope: str | None = None          # ← PATTERN PARAMETER
    status: str = "open"
    include_subjective: bool = True
    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    chronic: bool = False
    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    explain: bool = False
    plan: dict | None = None
    include_deferred: bool = False
    cluster: str | None = None
```

**Only `scope` and `status` affect pattern matching** — the rest control tier/count/sorting.

---

### **CRITICAL DIFFERENCES: SCOPE_MATCHES VS _MATCHES_PATTERN**

| Aspect | `scope_matches()` | `_matches_pattern()` |
|--------|-------------------|---------------------|
| **File** | `_work_queue/helpers.py:63` | `_state/filtering.py:176` |
| **Use Case** | Display (show/next/status) | Mutation (resolve/ignore) |
| **Wildcard Matching** | On ID, detector, file, dimension, summary | On ID only (not summary) |
| **Hash Suffix** | Yes (8+ hex chars) | No |
| **Subjective Dims** | Special handling (line 85-90) | None |
| **File Prefix** | Yes | Yes |
| **Detector Match** | Yes | Yes |

**Why two systems?** The work queue serves read-only views (can be flexible), while resolution needs strict, predictable matching to avoid accidental bulk mutations.

---

### **STATUS AND CHRONIC FILTERING**

- **Status filter** is separate from scope:
  - `status_matches(item_status, status_filter)` [helpers.py:32] checks "open"/"all"/"fixed" etc.
  - Applied **before** scope matching in the work queue pipeline
  
- **Chronic mode** (`--chronic` flag):
  - Returns only findings with `reopen_count >= 2`
  - Status filter forced to "open"
  - No tier fallback

---

### **SUMMARY TABLE: COMPLETE SCOPE FLOW**

| Function | File | Line | Purpose |
|----------|------|------|---------|
| `resolve_show_scope()` | show/scope.py | 107 | Parse CLI args → (ok, pattern, status, scope) |
| `load_matches()` | show/scope.py | 127 | Build queue with scope filter |
| `_build_dimension_lookup()` | show/scope.py | 14 | Create dimension → detector map |
| `load_matches_for_dimension()` | show/scope.py | 59 | Aggregate findings across multi-detector dimensions |
| `build_work_queue()` | _work_queue/core.py | 139 | Main orchestrator: calls build_finding_items + filters subjective |
| `build_finding_items()` | _work_queue/ranking.py | 28 | Create items, apply scope_matches filter |
| `scope_matches()` | _work_queue/helpers.py | 63 | **FIVE-BRANCH PATTERN MATCHER** |
| `match_findings()` | _state/resolution.py | 83 | Find findings matching pattern for resolve |
| `_matches_pattern()` | _state/filtering.py | 176 | **STATE PATTERN MATCHER** (resolve/ignore) |
| `status_matches()` | _work_queue/helpers.py | 32 | Filter by status (open/all/fixed/etc) |

---

### **KEY INSIGHTS FOR REFACTOR**

1. **Two parallel systems:** Work queue (show/next) vs. state (resolve/ignore) — they diverged for good reasons
2. **scope_matches has 5 branches:** Each handles a different user input style (wildcards, ID prefixes, hash suffixes, detector names, file paths)
3. **No cross-contamination:** show/status/next never call `_matches_pattern()`, and resolve never calls `scope_matches()`
4. **Dimension rewriting is show-only:** The dimension → detector lookup only exists in `show/scope.py`, not in the work queue layer
5. **Subjective items get special treatment:** They match on dimension_name/summary, not detector
6. **Hash suffix matching is a recent feature:** Lines 92-94 in scope_matches() support 8+ hex char suffixes for finding IDs

## SEMANTIC DISTINCTIONS FOUND

### 1. **Dimension-level check** (PRIMARY)
```python
"subjective_assessment" in dimension_data.get("detectors", {})
```
- Applied to: `dimension_scores[dimension_name]` dicts
- Files: 9 production files (results/core, state/scoring, scorecard_parts/dimensions, etc.)
- Semantics: Checks if a dimension was created by the subjective assessment engine

### 2. **Finding-level check** (DIFFERENT SEMANTIC)
```python
finding.get("detector") == "subjective_assessment"  # OR
finding.get("detector") == "subjective_review"       # OR
finding.get("detector") == "holistic_review"
```
- Applied to: Individual findings in state["findings"]
- Files: helpers.py, merge_findings.py, integrity.py
- Semantics: Checks if a specific finding came from the subjective review system
- **IMPORTANT**: A finding from detector="review" is NOT a "subjective" finding — only "subjective_review" counts

### 3. **Dimension display name fallback** (HYBRID)
```python
lowered_name in subjective_display_names  # OR
lowered_name in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()    # OR
lowered_name.startswith("elegance")
```
- Applied to: scorecard_projection.py, planning/render.py
- Semantics: Accepts dimension by display name even if detector check fails
- **IMPORTANT**: This is a fallback for handling display-name-based identification

### 4. **Detector list check** (DIFFERENT CONTEXT)
```python
{"subjective_assessment", "subjective_review"} & set(matched_dim.detectors)
```
- Applied to: Dimension objects from `scoring.py:DIMENSIONS`
- File: show/cmd.py
- Context: Checking if a Dimension (not dimension_scores entry) contains subjective detectors
- **NOTE**: This checks the SOURCE dimension definition, not the computed dimension_scores

---

## CONSISTENCY ASSESSMENT

**Are all checks semantically equivalent?**

**MOSTLY YES, with three important nuances**:

1. **Primary check is consistent**: 90% of code uses `"subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})`
   - All these are semantically identical and check the same thing

2. **Finding-level checks are DIFFERENT**: 
   - `is_subjective_finding()` checks finding.detector, not dimension_scores
   - This is intentional — distinguishes findings from dimension classification

3. **Display name fallback is intentional**:
   - `scorecard_subjective_entries()` accepts both detector key AND display name
   - This handles edge cases where name alone should identify subjectivity

4. **Dimension object checks are different**:
   - `show/cmd.py` checks the source DIMENSIONS list, not dimension_scores
   - Valid for checking "is this detector type subjective" rather than "is this dimension entry subjective"

---

## CRITICAL DEPENDENT LOCATIONS

Files that depend on this distinction being maintained:

1. **Scoring computation** (`_scoring/results/core.py`, `_state/scoring.py`)
   - Must filter correctly for weight application
   
2. **Scorecard rendering** (`app/output/scorecard_parts/dimensions.py`)
   - Must separate mechanical from subjective for display prioritization

3. **Work queue** (`_work_queue/helpers.py`, `_work_queue/ranking.py`)
   - Must force subjective items to tier 4

4. **Show command** (`app/commands/show/cmd.py`)
   - Must detect user's subjective dimension patterns

5. **Planning** (`engine/planning/render.py`, `engine/planning/common.py`)
   - Must separate dimensions for narrative and output formatting

## Complete Dimension Type System Analysis

I've conducted a thorough investigation of how dimensions are modeled, registered, stored, and queried throughout the desloppify codebase. Here's the complete picture:

---

### 1. **Core Dimension Type Definition**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/policy/core.py` (lines 15-19)

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Dimension:
    name: str
    tier: int
    detectors: list[str]
```

This is the **canonical immutable dimension type**. A dimension is:
- **name**: String like "Code quality", "File health", "Security", "Test health", "Duplication"
- **tier**: Integer (typically 3 or 4) indicating the refactoring effort tier
- **detectors**: List of detector names that roll up to this dimension

---

### 2. **Mechanical Dimensions Construction**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/policy/core.py` (lines 37-125)

**Key registry:** `DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES` (dict, lines 37-90)
- Maps detector name → `DetectorScoringPolicy` dataclass
- Each policy specifies: `detector`, `dimension`, `tier`, `file_based`, `use_loc_weight`, `excluded_zones`
- Examples:
  - `"unused"` → dimension: `"Code quality"`, tier: `3`
  - `"test_coverage"` → dimension: `"Test health"`, tier: `4`
  - `"security"` → dimension: `"Security"`, tier: `4`
  - `"concerns"` → dimension: `None`, tier: `None` (excluded from scoring)
  - `"review"` → dimension: `None`, tier: `None` (excluded from scoring)

**Automatic dimension building** via `_build_dimensions()` (lines 101-121):
- Iterates through `DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES`
- Groups detectors by `(dimension, tier)` pair
- Preserves first-seen order
- Produces `list[Dimension]`

**Exported singletons:**
- `DIMENSIONS` (list, line 124): All mechanical dimensions, in order of first-seen
- `DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME` (dict, line 125): Map from dimension name → `Dimension` object for O(1) lookup

**Dynamic registration:**
- `register_scoring_policy(policy: DetectorScoringPolicy)` (lines 210-213)
- Mutates `DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES`, then calls `_rebuild_derived()`
- `_rebuild_derived()` (lines 216-230) mutates `DIMENSIONS` and `DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME` in-place so all imports see updates

---

### 3. **Subjective Dimensions**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/subjective/core.py`

**Default dimensions source:**
- Line 99: Imported from `desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.holistic.DIMENSIONS`
- Holistic dimensions are pre-defined strings like:
  - `"high_level_elegance"`, `"mid_level_elegance"`, `"low_level_elegance"`
  - `"contract_coherence"`, `"type_safety"`, `"abstraction_fitness"`
  - `"cross_module_architecture"`, `"initialization_coupling"`, etc.

**Display name mapping:**
- `DISPLAY_NAMES` dict (lines 8-32) maps dimension keys to human-readable labels
- Example: `"high_level_elegance"` → `"High elegance"`
- Fallback via `_dimension_display_name()` (lines 60-70) that defers to review metadata

**How subjective dimensions get added to results:**
- Called by `append_subjective_dimensions()` (lines 85-249)
- Takes: `results` dict, `findings` dict, `assessments` dict, `failure_set`, optional `allowed_dimensions`
- Mutates `results` in-place, appending entries for each subjective dimension
- **Key logic:**
  - Line 99: Loads default holistic dimensions
  - Lines 117-134: Merges with real assessments (from review imports)
  - Lines 138-141: Adds assessed dimensions not in defaults
  - Lines 143-249: For each dimension, builds result entry with:
    - `"score"`: 0-100 float from assessment
    - `"tier"`: 4
    - `"checks"`: `SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS` (10, from policy/core.py line 181)
    - `"issues"`: Count of open review/concern findings for that dimension
    - `"detectors"`: Dict with single key `"subjective_assessment"` containing metadata

**Subjective detector structure** (lines 224-249):
```json
{
  "display_name": "...",
  "score": float (0-100),
  "tier": 4,
  "checks": 10,
  "issues": int,
  "detectors": {
    "subjective_assessment": {
      "potential": 10,
      "pass_rate": float,
      "issues": int,
      "weighted_failures": float,
      "assessment_score": float,
      "placeholder": bool,
      "dimension_key": str,
      "configured_weight": float,
      "components": [str],
      "component_scores": {str: float}  // optional
    }
  }
}
```

This is the **primary public API** for scoring. It re-exports:

From `engine._scoring.policy.core`:

- Constants: `SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS`, `MIN_SAMPLE`, `SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION`, `MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION`

- `register_scoring_policy()`, `matches_target_score()`

From `engine._scoring.subjective.core`:

From `engine._scoring.results.core`:

**Critical functions:**

### 5. **dimension_scores Storage in State**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/schema.py` (line 111)

dimension_scores: dict[str, dict[str, float]] | None

But the actual structure is richer. In `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/scoring.py` (lines 349-360):

```python
state["dimension_scores"] = {
    name: dict(
        score=lenient_scores[name]["score"],
        strict_score=strict_scores[name]["score"],
        verified_strict_score=verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
        checks=lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
        issues=lenient_scores[name]["issues"],

        detectors=lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
    )
    for name in lenient_scores
}
```

**Real dimension_scores entry structure:**
```json
{

    "strict_score": 90.5,
    "verified_strict_score": 90.5,
    "strict": 90.5,  // alias for strict_score (line 362)
    "checks": 42,
    "issues": 2,
    "tier": 3,
    "detectors": {

      "exports": {"potential": 8, "pass_rate": 0.875, "issues": 1, "weighted_failures": 0.125},
      ...
    }
  },
  "Test health": {
    "score": 75.0,
    "strict_score": 75.0,
    "verified_strict_score": 75.0,
    "strict": 75.0,
    "checks": 20,
    "issues": 5,
    "tier": 4,
    "detectors": {
      "test_coverage": {...},
      "subjective_review": {...}
    }
  },
  "Naming quality": {  // Subjective dimension
    "score": 0.0,
    "strict_score": 0.0,
    "verified_strict_score": 0.0,
    "strict": 0.0,
    "checks": 10,
    "issues": 0,
    "tier": 4,
    "detectors": {
      "subjective_assessment": {
        "potential": 10,
        "pass_rate": 0.0,
        "issues": 0,
        "weighted_failures": 10.0,
        "assessment_score": 0.0,
        "placeholder": true,
        "dimension_key": "naming_quality",
        "configured_weight": 2.0,
        "components": [],
        "component_scores": {}
      }
    }
  }
}
```

**Key observations:**
- Mechanical dimensions have `detectors` dict with multiple detector entries
- Subjective dimensions have single `detectors["subjective_assessment"]` entry
- Detection: `is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})`
- `carried_forward` flag (line 373) marks dims from previous scans not in current run
- Type annotation is overly simplified; actual values include nested objects

### 6. **Detector Registry & Dimension Linkage**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/core/registry.py`

`DetectorMeta` dataclass (lines 46-56):
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DetectorMeta:
    name: str
    display: str
    dimension: str  # Human dimension name like "Code quality"
    action_type: str
    guidance: str
    fixers: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    tool: str = ""
    structural: bool = False
    needs_judgment: bool = False
```

`DETECTORS` dict (lines 59-321): Maps detector name → `DetectorMeta`
- Example: `"unused"` → `DetectorMeta(name="unused", dimension="Code quality", action_type="auto_fix", ...)`
- **Note:** This `dimension` field in `DetectorMeta` is **human-readable** and **matches** the dimension names in `DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES`

`dimension_action_type(dim_name: str)` (lines 369-379):
- Takes a dimension name
- Finds all detectors with that dimension
- Returns the "best" action type across them (order: auto_fix < reorganize < refactor < manual_fix)

---

### 10. **Summary of Full Data Flow**

1. **Scan runs** → produces findings
2. **merge_scan()** merges findings into state
3. **_recompute_stats()** recalculates dimension scores
4. **_update_objective_health()** calls `compute_score_bundle()`
5. **compute_score_bundle()** calls `compute_dimension_scores_by_mode()`
6. **compute_dimension_scores_by_mode()** (results/core.py, lines 39-105):
   - Iterates `DIMENSIONS` (mechanical)
   - For each dimension, sums detector stats
   - Calls `append_subjective_dimensions()` to add subjective entries
   - Returns dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]] where inner dict matches dimension_scores structure
7. **State is updated** with new `dimension_scores`, aggregate scores computed via `compute_health_breakdown()`
8. **Narrative** reads `state["dimension_scores"]` for coaching
9. **CLI commands** (next, status, show, plan) render dimensions from state

This architecture cleanly separates **mechanical dimensions** (detector-based, automatic) from **subjective dimensions** (review-based, manual assessment), with both scoring systems integrated into a unified dimension_scores state object.

> DEVELOPER

I'm designing a refactor of the `show` command to fix a bug where mechanical dimensions (like Security, Duplication) are misclassified as subjective. The root cause is that the show command has no explicit entity resolution step — "dimension" is not a first-class concept in its pipeline. Here's the full context:

## Current Architecture

### The Show Pipeline
`cmd_show()` in `desloppify/app/commands/show/cmd.py`:
1. `resolve_show_scope(args)` → returns `(ok, pattern, status_filter, scope)` — scope = raw pattern, no translation
2. `load_matches(state, scope=scope, ...)` → builds work queue with `scope_matches()` filter. scope_matches only matches detector names, file paths, finding IDs
3. If no matches: 100-line fallback block (lines 99-213) tries to reinterpret:
   - `load_matches_for_dimension()` — looks up dimension's detectors, queries each
   - If still empty: ad-hoc subjective classification using DIMENSIONS, DISPLAY_NAMES, dimension_scores
   - Prints subjective message for ANY dimension with a display_name, including mechanical ones (THE BUG)

### Entity Types a Pattern Can Match
- **Detector name**: `"security"` (exact match via `scope_matches`)
- **Dimension name**: `"Security"`, `"security"`, `"file_health"` (not understood by scope_matches)  
- **File path**: `"src/auth.py"` (exact/prefix match)
- **Finding ID**: `"security::abc123"` (prefix or hash suffix)
- **Glob**: `"*.py"`, `"smells*"` (fnmatch on multiple fields)
- **Special views**: `"concerns"`, `"subjective"`

### Name Collision
The detector `"security"` and dimension `"Security"` share a name. `show security` currently matches the detector (lowercase exact match in scope_matches). This silently misses `cycles` findings that also belong to the Security dimension. When all `security`-detector findings are resolved but `cycles` findings remain, the fallback fires and misclassifies.

### Dimension Classification
- `Dimension` dataclass: `(name: str, tier: int, detectors: list[str])` — no subjective flag
- `MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS` dict: `{"file health": 2.0, "code quality": 1.0, "duplication": 1.0, "test health": 1.0, "security": 1.0}`
- `SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS` dict: `{"high elegance": 22.0, ...}` (12 entries)
- The check `"subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})` is inlined in 8+ files
- `DISPLAY_NAMES` dict is subjective-only (maps keys like `"cross_module_architecture"` → display labels)

### Key Files
- `desloppify/app/commands/show/cmd.py` — cmd_show (the buggy function)
- `desloppify/app/commands/show/scope.py` — resolve_show_scope, load_matches, load_matches_for_dimension, try_dimension_rewrite, _build_dimension_lookup
- `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` — scope_matches (5-branch pattern matcher), is_subjective_finding
- `desloppify/engine/_scoring/policy/core.py` — Dimension dataclass, DIMENSIONS, DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME, MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS, SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS
- `desloppify/engine/_scoring/subjective/core.py` — DISPLAY_NAMES (subjective only)
- `desloppify/scoring.py` — public API re-exports

## Design Goals

1. **Add explicit entity resolution** — before loading findings, determine what the user is asking about
2. **Centralize subjective classification** — one function, not 8+ inline checks
3. **Make dimensions first-class in the show pipeline** — dimension patterns should be resolved to their detector set early, not in an error-handling fallback
4. **Fix the bug** — mechanical dimensions must never be labeled subjective
5. **Minimal blast radius** — don't restructure the work queue or scoring system. Changes should be localized to the show command layer and a thin utility layer.

## Constraints
- Don't change the Dimension dataclass (it's frozen and used broadly)
- Don't change scope_matches in the work queue (used by next, status, resolve too)
- Don't change dimension_scores storage format
- Keep the fix localized: show/scope.py, show/cmd.py, and a small utility addition
- Existing tests in `desloppify/tests/` must pass

## What I Need
Design a concrete implementation plan with:
1. What new types/functions to add and where
2. How to restructure cmd_show's flow  
3. How to handle the detector-dimension name collision
4. What the "mechanical dimension with no findings" case should render
5. How to centralize is_subjective_dimension
6. Specific code changes per file

> AGENT

I'll start by thoroughly exploring the codebase to understand the current architecture before designing the plan.

   140→                    strict_val = dim_data.get("strict", score_val) if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else None
   141→                    if score_val is not None:
   142→                        print(colorize(
   143→                            f"  {display_name}: {score_val:.1f}% health (strict: {strict_val:.1f}%)",
   144→                            "bold",
   145→                        ))
   146→                    print(colorize(
   147→                        f"  '{pattern_raw.strip()}' is a subjective dimension — its score comes from design reviews, not code findings.",
   148→                        "yellow",
   149→                    ))
   150→                    # Count open review findings tagged with this dimension
   151→                    dim_reviews = [
   152→                        f for f in (state.get("findings") or {}).values()
   153→                        if f.get("detector") == "review" and f.get("status") == "open"
   154→                        and lowered in str(f.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   155→                    ]
   156→                    if dim_reviews:
   157→                        print(colorize(
   158→                            f"  {len(dim_reviews)} open review finding(s). Run `show review --status open`.",
   159→                            "dim",
   160→                        ))
   161→                    show_subjective_followup(
   162→                        state,
   163→                        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   164→                    )
   165→                    return
   166→
   167→            # "show subjective" with no findings still shows the subjective dashboard
   168→            is_subjective_view = pattern_raw and pattern_raw.strip().lower() in (
   169→                "subjective", "subjective_review",
   170→            )
   171→            if is_subjective_view:
   172→                print(colorize(f"No {status_filter} findings matching: {pattern}", "yellow"))
   173→                write_query(
   174→                    {
   175→                        "command": "show",
   176→                        "query": pattern,
   177→                        "status_filter": status_filter,
   178→                        "total": 0,
   179→                        "findings": [],
   180→                        "narrative": narrative,
   181→                    }
   182→                )
   183→                show_subjective_followup(
   184→                    state,
   185→                    target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   186→                )
   187→                return
   188→
   189→            hint = _detector_names_hint()
   190→            print(

   194→                )
   195→            )
   196→            print(

   199→                    f"Detectors: {hint}",

   201→                )
   202→            )
   203→            write_query(
   204→                {
   205→                    "command": "show",
   206→                    "query": pattern,
   207→                    "status_filter": status_filter,
   208→                    "total": 0,
   209→                    "findings": [],
   210→                    "narrative": narrative,
   211→                }
   212→            )
   213→            return
   214→
   215→    (
   216→        surfaced_matches,
   217→        hidden_by_detector,
   218→        noise_budget,
   219→        global_noise_budget,
   220→        budget_warning,
   221→    ) = resolve_noise(
   222→        config,
   223→        matches,
   224→    )
   225→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   226→
   227→    payload = build_show_payload(
   228→        surfaced_matches,
   229→        pattern,
   230→        status_filter,
   231→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   232→            total_matches=len(matches),
   233→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   234→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   235→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   236→        ),
   237→    )
   238→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   239→
   240→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   241→    if output_file:
   242→        if write_show_output_file(output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   243→            return
   244→        raise SystemExit(1)
   245→
   246→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20
   247→    render_findings(
   248→        surfaced_matches,
   249→        pattern=pattern,

   259→    try:

   261→        _plan = _load_plan()
   262→        _plan_active = _plan if (_plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")) else None

   269→    )
   270→
   271→    # Phase 5: naming guide for subjective views
   272→    if pattern_raw and pattern_raw.strip().lower() in ("subjective", "subjective_review"):
   273→        print(colorize("  Related views:", "dim"))

   275→        print(colorize("    `show subjective_review --status open`  Files needing re-review", "dim"))

1→"""Scope and queue helpers for show command selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     6→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     7→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     8→    QueueBuildOptions,
     9→    build_work_queue,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    12→
    13→
    14→def _build_dimension_lookup() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    15→    """Build a map from dimension name/key (lowered) to detector names."""
    16→    lookup: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    17→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    18→        detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
    19→        lookup[dim.name.lower()] = detectors
    20→        # Also index by underscore key: "file_health" -> detectors
    21→        key = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    22→        if key not in lookup:
    23→            lookup[key] = detectors
    24→    # Also add DISPLAY_NAMES reverse lookup (e.g. "abstraction_fit" -> "Abstraction Fit")
    25→    for key, display in scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        normalized_key = key.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    27→        normalized_display = display.lower()
    28→        # Find which dimension this belongs to via get_dimension_for_detector or direct name match
    29→        for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    30→            dim_lower = dim.name.lower()
    31→            if normalized_display == dim_lower or normalized_key == dim_lower.replace(" ", "_"):
    32→                detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
    33→                if normalized_key not in lookup:
    34→                    lookup[normalized_key] = detectors
    35→                if normalized_display not in lookup:
    36→                    lookup[normalized_display] = detectors
    37→    return lookup
    38→
    39→
    40→def try_dimension_rewrite(pattern: str) -> str | None:
    41→    """If pattern matches a dimension name/key, return a detector scope. Otherwise None."""
    42→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    43→    lowered = pattern.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    44→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered)
    45→    if not detectors:
    46→        # Try display name form (with spaces)
    47→        detectors = lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    48→    if not detectors:
    49→        return None
    50→    # Return first detector as scope — the work queue will match on detector name.
    51→    # For multi-detector dimensions, we can't pass multiple scopes through the current
    52→    # interface, but most dimensions have one primary detector. Use glob pattern.
    53→    if len(detectors) == 1:
    54→        return detectors[0]
    55→    # For multi-detector dimensions, use wildcard pattern that scope_matches handles
    56→    return None  # Fall through to load_matches_for_dimension
    57→
    58→
    59→def load_matches_for_dimension(
    60→    state: dict,
    61→    pattern: str,
    62→    *,
    63→    status_filter: str,
    64→) -> tuple[list[dict], str | None]:
    65→    """Try to load findings matching a dimension name. Returns (matches, rewritten_pattern)."""
    66→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    67→    lowered = pattern.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    68→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered) or lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    69→    if not detectors:
    70→        return [], None
    71→
    72→    # Load findings for each detector in this dimension
    73→    all_matches: list[dict] = []
    74→    for detector in detectors:
    75→        matches = load_matches(
    76→            state, scope=detector, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=False
    77→        )
    78→        all_matches.extend(matches)
    79→    # Deduplicate by finding ID
    80→    seen: set[str] = set()
    81→    unique: list[dict] = []
    82→    for item in all_matches:
    83→        fid = item.get("id", "")
    84→        if fid not in seen:
    85→            seen.add(fid)
    86→            unique.append(item)
    87→
    88→    # Find the display name for the dimension
    89→    dim_display = pattern
    90→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    91→        if dim.name.lower() == lowered or dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
    92→            dim_display = dim.name
    93→            break
    94→
    95→    return unique, dim_display
    96→
    97→
    98→def _detector_names_hint() -> str:
    99→    """Return a compact list of detector names for the help message."""
   100→    from desloppify.core import registry as registry_mod
   101→    names = getattr(registry_mod, "DISPLAY_ORDER", [])
   102→    if names:
   103→        return ", ".join(names[:10]) + (", ..." if len(names) > 10 else "")
   104→    return "smells, structural, security, review, ..."
   105→
   106→
   107→def resolve_show_scope(args) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str, str | None]:
   108→    """Resolve scope/pattern/status for a show invocation."""
   109→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   110→    pattern = args.pattern
   111→    status_filter = "open" if chronic else getattr(args, "status", "open")
   112→    if chronic:
   113→        scope = pattern
   114→        pattern = pattern or "<chronic>"
   115→        return True, pattern, status_filter, scope
   116→    if not pattern:
   117→        print(
   118→            colorize(
   119→                "Pattern required (or use --chronic). Try: desloppify show --help",
   120→                "yellow",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→        return False, None, status_filter, ""
   124→    return True, pattern, status_filter, pattern
   125→
   126→
   127→def load_matches(
   128→    state: dict,
   129→    *,
   130→    scope: str | None,
   131→    status_filter: str,
   132→    chronic: bool,
   133→) -> list[dict]:
   134→    """Load matching findings from the ranked queue."""
   135→    queue = build_work_queue(
   136→        state,
   137→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   138→            count=None,
   139→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   140→            scope=scope,
   141→            status=status_filter,
   142→            include_subjective=False,
   143→            chronic=chronic,
   144→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   145→        ),
   146→    )
   147→    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
   148→
   149→
   150→def resolve_noise(config: dict, matches: list[dict]):
   151→    """Apply detector/global noise budget to show matches."""
   152→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   153→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   154→    )
   155→    surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   156→        matches,
   157→        budget=noise_budget,
   158→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   159→    )
   160→    return (
   161→        surfaced_matches,
   162→        hidden_by_detector,
   163→        noise_budget,
   164→        global_noise_budget,
   165→        budget_warning,
   166→    )
   167→
   168→
   169→__all__ = [
   170→    "_detector_names_hint",
   171→    "load_matches",
   172→    "load_matches_for_dimension",
   173→    "resolve_noise",
   174→    "resolve_show_scope",
   175→]
   176→
