```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   169→
   170→
   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:
   174→        return {}
   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:
   179→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   180→        if not name:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
   294→
   295→
   296→__all__ = [
   297→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   298→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   299→    "build_subjective_items",
   300→    "detail_dict",
```

```
   100→
   101→def _build_dimensions() -> list[Dimension]:
   102→    """Derive dimensions from DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.
   103→
   104→    Each unique (dimension, tier) pair becomes a Dimension, with its detectors
   105→    collected automatically. Order follows first-seen in the policies dict.
   106→    """
   107→    # Collect (dimension_name -> tier) preserving first-seen order,
   108→    # and group detectors by dimension.
   109→    dim_tiers: dict[str, int] = {}
   110→    grouped: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   111→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items():
   112→        if policy.dimension is None or policy.tier is None:
   113→            continue
   114→        if policy.dimension not in dim_tiers:
   115→            dim_tiers[policy.dimension] = policy.tier
   116→            grouped[policy.dimension] = []
   117→        grouped[policy.dimension].append(detector)
   118→    return [
   119→        Dimension(name=name, tier=tier, detectors=grouped[name])
   120→        for name, tier in dim_tiers.items()
   121→    ]
   122→
   123→
   124→DIMENSIONS = _build_dimensions()
   125→DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME = {d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS}
   126→
   127→TIER_WEIGHTS = {
   128→    Tier.AUTO_FIX: 1,
   129→    Tier.QUICK_FIX: 2,
   130→    Tier.JUDGMENT: 3,
   131→    Tier.MAJOR_REFACTOR: 4,
   132→}
   133→CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS = {Confidence.HIGH: 1.0, Confidence.MEDIUM: 0.7, Confidence.LOW: 0.3}
   134→
   135→# Minimum checks for full dimension weight — below this, weight is dampened
   136→# proportionally. Prevents small-sample dimensions from swinging the overall score.
   137→MIN_SAMPLE = 200
   138→
   139→# Holistic review weight: findings with file="." and detail.holistic=True
   140→# get a 10x weight multiplier for display/priority purposes (issues list,
   141→# remediation engine).  NOT used in score computation — review findings are
   142→# excluded from the detection scoring pipeline (scored via assessments only).
   143→HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER = 10.0
   144→HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL = 10
   145→
   146→# Budget: subjective dimensions get this fraction of the overall score.
   147→# Mechanical dimensions get the remainder.
   148→SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.60
   149→MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 1.0 - SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   150→
   151→# Per-dimension weighting within the mechanical pool.
   152→# Keep this balanced: no special boost for security/test in the pool itself.
   153→MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   154→    "file health": 2.0,
   155→    "code quality": 1.0,
   156→    "duplication": 1.0,
   157→    "test health": 1.0,
   158→    "security": 1.0,
   159→}
   160→
   161→# Per-dimension weighting within the subjective pool.
   162→# Emphasize elegance and contract/type coherence for a stronger architecture north star.
   163→SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   164→    "high elegance": 22.0,
   165→    "mid elegance": 22.0,
   166→    "low elegance": 12.0,
   167→    "contracts": 12.0,
   168→    "type safety": 12.0,
   169→    "abstraction fit": 8.0,
   170→    "logic clarity": 6.0,
   171→    # Low-but-meaningful structural signal (about half of the subjective
   172→    # average weight) so it matters without dominating craftsmanship axes.
   173→    "structure nav": 5.0,
   174→    "error consistency": 3.0,
   175→    "naming quality": 2.0,
   176→    "ai generated debt": 1.0,
   177→    "design coherence": 10.0,
   178→}
   179→
   180→# Synthetic check count for subjective dimensions in dimension_scores.
   181→SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS = 10
   182→
   183→FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE: dict[ScoreMode, frozenset[str]] = {
   184→    "lenient": frozenset({"open"}),
   185→    "strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix"}),
   186→    "verified_strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "fixed", "false_positive"}),
   187→}
   188→
   189→# Tolerance for treating a subjective score as "on target" in integrity checks.
   190→# Scores within this band of the target are flagged as potential gaming.
   191→SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE = 0.05
   192→
   193→
   194→def matches_target_score(
   195→    score: object,
   196→    target: object,
   197→    *,
   198→    tolerance: float = SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
   199→) -> bool:
   200→    """Return True when score is within tolerance of target."""
   201→    try:
   202→        score_value = float(score)
   203→        target_value = float(target)
   204→        tolerance_value = max(0.0, float(tolerance))
   205→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   206→        return False
   207→    return abs(score_value - target_value) <= tolerance_value
   208→
   209→
   210→def register_scoring_policy(policy: DetectorScoringPolicy) -> None:
   211→    """Register a scoring policy at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   212→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES[policy.detector] = policy
   213→    _rebuild_derived()
   214→
   215→
   216→def _rebuild_derived() -> None:
   217→    """Rebuild DIMENSIONS, DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME, FILE_BASED_DETECTORS from current state.
   218→
   219→    Mutates existing objects in-place so that all references (including imports
   220→    that bound the original objects) see the updates.
   221→    """
   222→    new_dims = _build_dimensions()
   223→    DIMENSIONS.clear()
   224→    DIMENSIONS.extend(new_dims)
   225→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.clear()
   226→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.update({d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS})
   227→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.clear()
   228→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.update(
   229→        det for det, pol in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items() if pol.file_based
   230→    )
   231→
   232→
   233→def detector_policy(detector: str) -> DetectorScoringPolicy:
   234→    """Get scoring policy for a detector, with a safe default fallback."""
   235→    return DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(
   236→        detector,
   237→        DetectorScoringPolicy(detector=detector, dimension=None, tier=None),
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→__all__ = [
   242→    "CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS",
   243→    "DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES",
   244→    "DIMENSIONS",
   245→    "DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME",
   246→    "FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE",
   247→    "FILE_BASED_DETECTORS",
   248→    "HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER",
```

```
1→"""Subjective-dimension scoring helpers."""
     2→

     6→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS
     7→
     8→DISPLAY_NAMES: dict[str, str] = {
     9→    # Holistic dimensions

    11→    "initialization_coupling": "Init coupling",
    12→    "convention_outlier": "Convention drift",

    33→
    34→def _display_fallback(dim_name: str) -> str:
    35→    words = dim_name.replace("_", " ")
    36→    return words[0].upper() + words[1:] if words else words
    37→
    38→
    39→def _normalize_dimension_key(dim_name: object) -> str:
    40→    if not isinstance(dim_name, str):
    41→        return ""
    42→    return "_".join(dim_name.strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())
    43→
    44→
    45→def _primary_lang_from_findings(findings: dict) -> str | None:
    46→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    47→    for finding in findings.values():
    48→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
    49→            continue
    50→        raw_lang = finding.get("lang")
    51→        if not isinstance(raw_lang, str) or not raw_lang.strip():
    52→            continue
    53→        key = raw_lang.strip().lower()
    54→        counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    55→    if not counts:
    56→        return None
    57→    return max(counts, key=counts.get)
    58→
    59→
    60→def _dimension_display_name(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> str:
    61→    try:
    62→        # Deferred import to avoid circular: scoring -> _scoring/results/core
    63→        # -> _scoring/subjective/core -> intelligence.review -> state -> scoring
    64→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    65→            dimension_display_name,
    66→        )
    67→
    68→        return str(dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    69→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    70→        return DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim_name, _display_fallback(dim_name))
    71→
    72→
    73→def _dimension_weight(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> float:
    74→    try:
    75→        # Deferred import to avoid circular (see _dimension_display_name).
    76→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    77→            dimension_weight,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→        return float(dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    81→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    82→        return 1.0
    83→
    84→
    85→def append_subjective_dimensions(
    86→    results: dict,
    87→    findings: dict,
    88→    assessments: dict | None,
    89→    failure_set: frozenset[str],
    90→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    91→) -> None:
    92→    """Append subjective review dimensions to results dict (mutates results).
    93→
    94→    Subjective scoring is evidence-first: open review findings for a dimension
    95→    determine pass-rate, while imported assessment scores are retained as
    96→    metadata for transparency.
    97→    """
    98→    # Deferred import to avoid circular (see _dimension_display_name).
    99→    from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.holistic import DIMENSIONS
   100→
   101→    raw_defaults = DIMENSIONS
   102→    allowed = (
   103→        {_normalize_dimension_key(name) for name in allowed_dimensions}
   104→        if allowed_dimensions is not None
   105→        else None
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    default_dimensions = []
   109→    for raw_dim in raw_defaults:
   110→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
   111→        if not dim:
   112→            continue
   113→        if allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
   114→            continue
   115→        default_dimensions.append(dim)
   116→
   117→    assessed: dict[str, dict] = {}
   118→    for raw_dim, payload in (assessments or {}).items():
   119→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
   120→        if not dim:
   121→            continue
   122→        # Real assessments (from review imports) always count regardless of
   123→        # allowed_dimensions — they were deliberately imported and should not
   124→        # be silently discarded.  However, system-generated placeholders
   125→        # (scan_reset_subjective) are not real assessments and should respect
   126→        # the allowed_dimensions scope like defaults.
   127→        is_placeholder = isinstance(payload, dict) and (
   128→            payload.get("placeholder") is True
   129→            or payload.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   130→            or payload.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   131→        )
   132→        if is_placeholder and allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
   133→            continue
   134→        assessed[dim] = payload
   135→    existing_lower = {k.lower() for k in results}
   136→    lang_name = _primary_lang_from_findings(findings)
   137→
   138→    all_dims = list(default_dimensions)
   139→    for dim_name in assessed:
   140→        if dim_name not in default_dimensions:
   141→            all_dims.append(dim_name)
   142→
   143→    for dim_name in all_dims:
   144→        is_default = dim_name in default_dimensions
   145→        assessment = assessed.get(dim_name)
   146→        has_assessment = isinstance(assessment, dict)
   147→        if not is_default and not assessment:
   148→            continue
   149→
   150→        display = _dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   151→        if display.lower() in existing_lower:
   152→            display = f"{display} (subjective)"
   153→
   154→        # Count open review/concern findings for display (work queue), but
   155→        # these do NOT drive the dimension score — only assessment scores do.
   156→        issue_count = sum(
   157→            1
   158→            for finding in findings.values()
   159→            if finding.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   160→            and finding.get("status") in failure_set
   161→            and _normalize_dimension_key(
   162→                finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension")
   163→            )
   164→            == dim_name
   165→        )
   166→
   167→        assessment_score = (
   168→            max(0.0, min(100.0, float(assessment.get("score", 0))))
   169→            if isinstance(assessment, dict)
   170→            else 0.0
   171→        )
   172→        integrity_penalty = (
   173→            assessment.get("integrity_penalty")
   174→            if isinstance(assessment, dict)
   175→            else None
   176→        )
   177→        reset_pending = bool(
   178→            isinstance(assessment, dict)
   179→            and (
   180→                assessment.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   181→                or assessment.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   182→                or assessment.get("placeholder") is True
   183→            )
   184→        )
   185→        if reset_pending:
   186→            score = 0.0
   187→            pass_rate = 0.0
   188→        elif integrity_penalty == "target_match_reset":
   189→            score = 0.0
   190→            pass_rate = 0.0
   191→        elif has_assessment:
   192→            # Assessment score drives the dimension score directly.
   193→            # Resolving review findings does NOT change this score —
   194→            # only a fresh review import updates it.
   195→            score = assessment_score
   196→            pass_rate = score / 100.0
   197→        else:
   198→            # No assessment yet: explicit unassessed placeholder.
   199→            # Subjective dimensions start at 0 until an assessment is imported.
   200→            score = 0.0
   201→            pass_rate = 0.0
   202→        components: list[str] = []
   203→        component_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   204→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
   205→            raw_components = assessment.get("components")
   206→            if isinstance(raw_components, list):
   207→                components = [
   208→                    str(item).strip()
   209→                    for item in raw_components
   210→                    if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
   211→                ]
   212→            raw_component_scores = assessment.get("component_scores")
   213→            if isinstance(raw_component_scores, dict):
   214→                for key, value in raw_component_scores.items():
   215→                    if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
   216→                        continue
   217→                    if not is_numeric(value):
   218→                        continue
   219→                    component_scores[key.strip()] = round(
   220→                        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))),
   221→                        1,
   222→                    )
   223→
   224→        results[display] = {
   225→            "score": round(float(score), 1),
   226→            "tier": 4,
   227→            "checks": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   228→            "issues": issue_count,
   229→            "detectors": {
   230→                "subjective_assessment": {
   231→                    "potential": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   232→                    "pass_rate": round(pass_rate, 4),
   233→                    "issues": issue_count,
   234→                    "weighted_failures": round(SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS * (1 - pass_rate), 4),
   235→                    "assessment_score": round(assessment_score, 1),
   236→                    "placeholder": reset_pending or not has_assessment,
   237→                    "dimension_key": dim_name,
   238→                    "configured_weight": round(
   239→                        _dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name), 6
   240→                    ),
   241→                    "components": components,
   242→                    **(
   243→                        {"component_scores": component_scores}
   244→                        if component_scores
   245→                        else {}
   246→                    ),
   247→                }
   248→            },
   249→        }
   250→
   251→
   252→__all__ = [
   253→    "DISPLAY_NAMES",
   254→    "append_subjective_dimensions",
   255→]
   256→
```

```
1→"""Rendering and output helpers for show command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_ranked_actions
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.subjective import print_subjective_followup
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.scan import (
    11→    scan_reporting_dimensions as reporting_dimensions_mod,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→from desloppify.core.paths_api import read_code_snippet
    15→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    16→from desloppify.core.enums import canonical_finding_status
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    19→
    20→from .formatting import format_detail
    21→
    22→
    23→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    24→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    25→    try:
    26→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    27→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} findings to {output_file}", "green"))
    28→    except OSError as exc:
    29→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    30→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    31→        return False
    32→    return True
    33→
    34→
    35→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    36→    """Group findings by file and sort by descending count."""
    37→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    38→    for finding in matches:
    39→        by_file[finding["file"]].append(finding)
    40→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    41→
    42→
    43→def _print_single_finding(finding: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    44→    """Render a single finding to terminal."""
    45→    normalized_status = canonical_finding_status(finding.get("status"))
    46→    status_icon = {
    47→        "open": "○",
    48→        "fixed": "✓",
    49→        "wontfix": "—",
    50→        "false_positive": "✗",
    51→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    52→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    53→    zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    54→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    55→    print(
    56→        f"    {status_icon} T{finding['tier']} [{finding['confidence']}] {finding['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    57→    )
    58→
    59→    detail_parts = format_detail(finding.get("detail", {}))
    60→    if detail_parts:
    61→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    62→    if show_code:
    63→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    64→        target_line = (
    65→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    66→        )
    67→        if target_line and finding["file"] not in (".", ""):
    68→            snippet = read_code_snippet(finding["file"], target_line)
    69→            if snippet:
    70→                print(snippet)
    71→    if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    72→        print(
    73→            colorize(
    74→                f"      ⟳ reopened {finding['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    75→                "red",
    76→            )
    77→        )
    78→    if finding.get("note"):
    79→        print(colorize(f"      note: {finding['note']}", "dim"))
    80→    print(colorize(f"      {finding['id']}", "dim"))
    81→
    82→
    83→def render_findings(
    84→    matches: list[dict],
    85→    *,
    86→    pattern: str,
    87→    status_filter: str,
    88→    show_code: bool,
    89→    top: int,
    90→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    91→    hidden_total: int,
    92→    noise_budget: int,
    93→    global_noise_budget: int,
    94→    budget_warning: str | None,
    95→) -> None:
    96→    """Render grouped findings and rollup summary to terminal."""
    97→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
    98→    print(
    99→        colorize(
   100→            f"\n  {len(matches)} {status_filter} findings matching '{pattern}'\n",
   101→            "bold",
   102→        )
   103→    )
   104→    if budget_warning:
   105→        print(colorize(f"  {budget_warning}\n", "yellow"))
   106→    if hidden_total:
   107→        global_label = (
   108→            f", {global_noise_budget} global" if global_noise_budget > 0 else ""
   109→        )
   110→        hidden_parts = ", ".join(
   111→            f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   112→        )
   113→        print(
   114→            colorize(
   115→                (
   116→                    "  Noise budget: "
   117→                    f"{noise_budget}/detector{global_label} "
   118→                    f"({hidden_total} hidden: {hidden_parts})\n"
   119→                ),
   120→                "dim",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→    shown_files = sorted_files[:top]
   125→    remaining_files = sorted_files[top:]
   126→    remaining_findings = sum(len(files) for _, files in remaining_files)
   127→
   128→    for filepath, findings in shown_files:
   129→        findings.sort(
   130→            key=lambda finding: (
   131→                finding["tier"],
   132→                CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding["confidence"], 9),
   133→            )
   134→        )
   135→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   136→        print(
   137→            colorize(f"  {display_path}", "cyan")
   138→            + colorize(f"  ({len(findings)} findings)", "dim")
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        for finding in findings:
   142→            _print_single_finding(finding, show_code=show_code)
   143→        print()
   144→
   145→    if remaining_findings:
   146→        print(
   147→            colorize(
   148→                (
   149→                    f"  ... and {len(remaining_files)} more files "
   150→                    f"({remaining_findings} findings). "
   151→                    f"Use --top {top + 20} to see more.\n"
   152→                ),
   153→                "dim",
   154→            )
   155→        )
   156→
   157→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   158→    by_tier: dict[int, int] = defaultdict(int)
   159→    for finding in matches:
   160→        by_detector[finding["detector"]] += 1
   161→        by_tier[finding["tier"]] += 1
   162→
   163→    print(colorize("  Summary:", "bold"))
   164→    print(
   165→        colorize(
   166→            (
   167→                "    By tier:     "
   168→                + ", ".join(
   169→                    f"T{tier}:{count}" for tier, count in sorted(by_tier.items())
   170→                )
   171→            ),
   172→            "dim",
   173→        )
   174→    )
   175→    print(
   176→        colorize(
   177→            (
   178→                "    By detector: "
   179→                + ", ".join(
   180→                    f"{detector}:{count}"
   181→                    for detector, count in sorted(
   182→                        by_detector.items(), key=lambda item: -item[1]
   183→                    )
   184→                )
   185→            ),
   186→            "dim",
   187→        )
   188→    )
   189→    if hidden_total:
   190→        print(
   191→            colorize(
   192→                (
   193→                    "    Hidden:      "
   194→                    + ", ".join(
   195→                        f"{detector}:+{count}"
   196→                        for detector, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   197→                    )
   198→                ),
   199→                "dim",
   200→            )
   201→        )
   202→    print()
   203→
   204→
   205→def show_agent_plan(
   206→    narrative: dict, matches: list[dict], *, plan: dict | None = None
   207→) -> None:
   208→    """Render a compact plan from current findings and narrative actions.
   209→
   210→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead.
   211→    """
   212→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
   213→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan as _pap
   214→        _pap(
   215→            [],
   216→            plan=plan,
   217→            header="  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next` to see your next task):",
   218→        )
   219→        return
   220→
   221→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   222→    if not actions and not matches:
   223→        return
   224→
   225→    print(
   226→        colorize(
   227→            "  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next --count 20` to inspect more items):",
   228→            "yellow",
   229→        )
   230→    )
   231→    if actions:
   232→        top = actions[0]
   233→        print(
   234→            colorize(
   235→                f"  Agent focus: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}",
   236→                "cyan",
   237→            )
   238→        )
   239→    elif matches:
   240→        first = matches[0]
   241→        print(
   242→            colorize(
   243→                "  Agent focus: `desloppify next --count 20` — "
   244→                f"inspect and resolve `{first.get('id', '')}`",
   245→                "cyan",
   246→            )
   247→        )
   248→
   249→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   250→        print()
   251→
   252→
   253→def show_subjective_followup(state: dict, target_strict_score: float) -> None:
   254→    """Show subjective follow-up guidance for the current state."""
   255→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   256→    if not dim_scores:
   257→        return
   258→
   259→    subjective = reporting_dimensions_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries(
   260→        state,
   261→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   262→    )
   263→    if not subjective:
   264→        return
   265→
   266→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   267→        state,
   268→        subjective,
   269→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   270→        max_quality_items=3,
   271→        max_integrity_items=5,
   272→    )
   273→    if print_subjective_followup(followup):
   274→        print()
   275→
```

```
1→"""Payload builders for show command query/output serialization."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→
     8→
     9→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    10→class ShowPayloadMeta:
    11→    total_matches: int | None = None
    12→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int] | None = None
    13→    noise_budget: int | None = None
    14→    global_noise_budget: int | None = None
    15→
    16→
    17→def build_show_payload(
    18→    matches: list[dict],
    19→    pattern: str,
    20→    status_filter: str,
    21→    meta: ShowPayloadMeta | None = None,
    22→) -> dict:
    23→    """Build the structured JSON payload shared by query file and --output."""
    24→    resolved_meta = meta or ShowPayloadMeta()
    25→
    26→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    27→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    28→    by_tier: dict[int, int] = defaultdict(int)
    29→    for finding in matches:
    30→        by_file[finding["file"]].append(finding)
    31→        by_detector[finding["detector"]] += 1
    32→        by_tier[finding["tier"]] += 1
    33→
    34→    payload = {
    35→        "query": pattern,
    36→        "status_filter": status_filter,
    37→        "total": len(matches),
    38→        "summary": {
    39→            "by_tier": {f"T{tier}": count for tier, count in sorted(by_tier.items())},
    40→            "by_detector": dict(sorted(by_detector.items(), key=lambda item: -item[1])),
    41→            "files": len(by_file),
    42→        },
    43→        "by_file": {
    44→            fp: [
    45→                {
    46→                    "id": f["id"],
    47→                    "tier": f["tier"],
    48→                    "confidence": f["confidence"],
    49→                    "summary": f["summary"],
    50→                    "detail": f.get("detail", {}),
    51→                }
    52→                for f in fs
    53→            ]
    54→            for fp, fs in sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[1]))
    55→        },
    56→    }
```

```
    23→class TestFormatDetail:
    24→    """format_detail builds display-ready parts from a finding detail dict."""
    25→
    26→    def test_empty_detail(self):
    27→        assert format_detail({}) == []
    28→
    29→    def test_simple_string_fields(self):
    30→        parts = format_detail({"category": "imports", "kind": "default"})
    31→        assert "category: imports" in parts
    32→        assert "kind: default" in parts
    33→
    34→    def test_line_number(self):
    35→        parts = format_detail({"line": 42})
    36→        assert "line: 42" in parts
    37→
    38→    def test_lines_list_truncated(self):
    39→        parts = format_detail({"lines": [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]})
    40→        # Only first 5 should appear
    41→        lines_part = [p for p in parts if p.startswith("lines:")]
    42→        assert len(lines_part) == 1
    43→        assert "6" not in lines_part[0]
    44→        assert "1" in lines_part[0]
    45→
    46→    def test_signals_list(self):
    47→        parts = format_detail({"signals": ["a", "b", "c", "d"]})
    48→        sig_part = [p for p in parts if p.startswith("signals:")][0]
    49→        # Only first 3
    50→        assert "a" in sig_part
    51→        assert "c" in sig_part
    52→        assert "d" not in sig_part
    53→
    54→    def test_importers_zero_is_shown(self):
    55→        """importers=0 is meaningful and should be displayed."""

    57→        assert "importers: 0" in parts
    58→
    59→    def test_importers_none_is_hidden(self):

   151→class TestSuppressedMatchEstimate:
   152→    def _make_finding(self, fid, *, file="a.ts", detector="unused",
   153→                      tier=2, confidence="high"):
   154→        return {
   155→            "id": fid, "file": file, "detector": detector,
   156→            "tier": tier, "confidence": confidence,
   157→            "summary": f"Finding {fid}", "detail": {},
   158→        }

   160→    def test_detector_name(self):

   165→
   166→    def test_unknown_pattern_returns_zero(self):
   167→        assert suppressed_match_estimate("nope", {"smells": 3}) == 0

   169→    def test_single_finding(self):
   170→        findings = [self._make_finding("unused::a.ts::foo")]
   171→        result = build_show_payload(findings, "a.ts", "open")
   172→        assert result["total"] == 1
   173→        assert result["summary"]["files"] == 1
   174→        assert result["summary"]["by_tier"] == {"T2": 1}
   175→        assert result["summary"]["by_detector"] == {"unused": 1}
   176→        assert "a.ts" in result["by_file"]

   178→
   179→    def test_multiple_files_and_detectors(self):
   180→        findings = [
   181→            self._make_finding(
   182→                "unused::a.ts::foo", file="a.ts", detector="unused", tier=2
   183→            ),
   184→            self._make_finding(
   185→                "smells::b.ts::bar", file="b.ts", detector="smells", tier=3
   186→            ),
   187→            self._make_finding(
   188→                "unused::a.ts::baz", file="a.ts", detector="unused", tier=2
   189→            ),
   190→        ]
   191→        result = build_show_payload(findings, "*", "open")
   192→        assert result["total"] == 3
   193→        assert result["summary"]["files"] == 2
   194→        assert result["summary"]["by_tier"] == {"T2": 2, "T3": 1}
   195→        assert result["summary"]["by_detector"]["unused"] == 2
   196→        assert result["summary"]["by_detector"]["smells"] == 1
   197→
   198→    def test_by_file_sorted_by_count_descending(self):

   201→            self._make_finding("a2", file="a.ts"),
   202→            self._make_finding("a3", file="a.ts"),

   220→    def test_payload_includes_hidden_metadata_when_provided(self):
```

```
    19→class TestHashSuffixMatching:
    20→    def test_hash_suffix_matches(self):
    21→        item = _item(id="smells::a.py::1497da0b")
    22→        assert scope_matches(item, "1497da0b") is True
    23→
    24→    def test_hash_suffix_no_match(self):
    25→        item = _item(id="smells::a.py::1497da0b")

    27→
    28→    def test_hash_suffix_case_insensitive(self):
    29→        item = _item(id="smells::a.py::1497da0b")
    30→        assert scope_matches(item, "1497DA0B") is True
    31→
    32→    def test_short_hex_not_treated_as_hash(self):
    33→        """7-char hex falls through to other matching (not hash suffix)."""

    35→        # 7-char hex is too short for hash matching
    36→        assert scope_matches(item, "1497da0") is False
    37→
    38→    def test_detector_name_still_works(self):
    39→        item = _item(id="smells::a.py::abcdef01", detector="smells")
    40→        assert scope_matches(item, "smells") is True
```

```
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
```

```
1→"""Scorecard dimension row helpers for the engine/planning layer.
     2→
     3→Provides ``scorecard_dimension_rows`` without importing app-layer modules.
     4→The app scorecard surface re-exports this so external call-sites are unaffected.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from desloppify.scoring import DIMENSIONS
    10→
    11→
    12→def scorecard_dimension_rows(
    13→    state: dict,
    14→    *,
    15→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    16→) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
    17→    """Return scorecard rows using canonical dimension ordering.
    18→
    19→    Tries ``prepare_scorecard_dimensions`` from the app layer when available
    20→    (deferred import to avoid hard engine -> app dependency), then falls back
    21→    to a simple mechanical-dimension listing.
    22→    """
    23→    if dim_scores is None:
    24→        dim_scores = (
    25→            state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if isinstance(state, dict) else {}
    26→        )
    27→        projected_state = state
    28→    else:
    29→        projected_state = dict(state)
    30→        projected_state["dimension_scores"] = dim_scores
    31→
    32→    # Try the full app-layer scorecard projection when it can be imported.
    33→    try:
    34→        from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimensions import (
    35→            prepare_scorecard_dimensions,
    36→        )
    37→
    38→        rows = prepare_scorecard_dimensions(projected_state)
    39→        if rows:
    40→            return rows
    41→    except ImportError:
    42→        pass
    43→
    44→    # Fallback for synthetic/unit-test states without full scorecard context.
    45→    fallback_dim_scores = dim_scores or {}
    46→    if not isinstance(fallback_dim_scores, dict):
    47→        return []
    48→
    49→    mechanical_names = [dimension.name for dimension in DIMENSIONS]
    50→    fallback_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
```

> AGENT

This is very revealing. The dimension lookup has `"security": ["security", "cycles"]` but `scope_matches` will match `"security"` as a detector name first (exact match on line 97: `detector == scope`). So `show security` hits the detector path, only gets `security`-detector findings, misses `cycles` findings. When the `security` detector findings are all resolved but `cycles` findings remain, the fallback fires and we end up in the subjective classification code.

Let me verify the exact bug path one more time.

## Implementation Plan: Fix Mechanical Dimension Misclassification in `show` Command

### 1. Root Cause Analysis

The bug occurs in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/cmd.py` at lines 99-165. When a user runs `show security`:

1. `load_matches(state, scope="security")` passes `"security"` to the work queue, where `scope_matches` matches it as a **detector name** (exact match: `detector == scope` at helpers.py line 97). This only returns findings where `detector == "security"`, missing `cycles` findings that also belong to the Security dimension.

2. If all `security`-detector findings are resolved but `cycles` findings remain, `matches` is empty.

3. The fallback at line 101 calls `load_matches_for_dimension(state, "security")`, which looks up `"security"` in the dimension lookup and queries both `security` and `cycles` detectors. If only `cycles` findings remain, this returns findings. **This path works correctly.**

4. However, if ALL findings for the dimension are resolved (both `security` and `cycles`), `dim_matches` is also empty. Execution falls through to line 108.

5. At lines 112-113, `dim_lookup.get("security")` finds the `Dimension(name="Security", detectors=["security", "cycles"])`. At line 116, the subjective-detector check passes (Security's detectors are not subjective), so `matched_dim` stays non-None.

6. At line 120, `DISPLAY_NAMES.get("security")` returns `None` (DISPLAY_NAMES only has subjective dimension keys). But at line 123, the code iterates `dimension_scores` keys, finds `"Security"` (which normalizes to `"security"`), and sets `display_name = "Security"`.

7. At line 128, `display_name` is truthy, so the code enters the block and prints **"'security' is a subjective dimension"**. This is the bug -- a mechanical dimension with zero open findings is labeled subjective.

The same bug affects ANY mechanical dimension name that exists in `dimension_scores`: `"file_health"`, `"code_quality"`, `"duplication"`, `"test_health"`, `"security"`.

### 2. Design: Resolved Entity Types

Introduce a lightweight `ResolvedEntity` type that makes the entity resolution step explicit. This replaces the implicit multi-branch fallback in `cmd_show`.

```python
# In show/scope.py

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

EntityKind = Literal[
    "detector",         # Pattern matched a detector name
    "dimension",        # Pattern matched a dimension name/key
    "file_or_pattern",  # Pattern matched a file path, glob, or finding ID
    "special_view",     # "concerns", "subjective"
    "unknown",          # No match found
]

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ResolvedEntity:
    kind: EntityKind
    pattern: str              # Original user pattern
    display_name: str         # Human-readable label for output
    detectors: list[str]      # Detector names (for dimension/detector kinds)
    is_subjective: bool       # Whether this is a subjective dimension/detector
```

### 3. Centralized Subjective Classification

Add a single function to `show/scope.py` (or a small utility):

```python
def is_dimension_score_subjective(dim_data: dict) -> bool:
    """Authoritative check: is a dimension_scores entry subjective?

    A dimension is subjective if and only if its detectors dict contains
    the 'subjective_assessment' key. This is the ONLY correct check.
    Mechanical dimensions (File health, Code quality, Duplication,
    Test health, Security) never have this detector.
    """
    return "subjective_assessment" in (dim_data.get("detectors") or {})
```

This function belongs in the scoring policy layer at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/policy/core.py` and should be re-exported through `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/scoring.py`. However, per the constraint of minimal blast radius, I recommend adding it to `show/scope.py` first and, as a follow-up, migrating callers. The 6 existing inline checks can be migrated incrementally later.

For the show command fix, the function can be local to `show/scope.py` since it only needs to be called there.

### 4. Entity Resolution Function

Add to `show/scope.py`:

```python
def resolve_entity(pattern: str, state: dict) -> ResolvedEntity:
    """Determine what entity a user pattern refers to.

    Resolution priority:
    1. Special views: "concerns", "subjective"
    2. Dimension name (case-insensitive, underscore-tolerant)
       - Both mechanical and subjective dimensions
       - Handles the "security" collision by preferring dimension over detector
    3. Detector name (exact match)
    4. File path, glob, finding ID (passthrough to scope_matches)
    5. Unknown
    """
```

The key design decision: **dimension takes priority over detector when names collide**. When a user types `show security`, they almost certainly mean the Security dimension (all related findings), not just the `security` detector subset. This is the correct behavior because:
- The dimension is the user-facing concept
- The detector is an implementation detail
- Showing all findings for a dimension is strictly more useful than showing a subset

Implementation:

```python
def resolve_entity(pattern: str, state: dict) -> ResolvedEntity:
    if not pattern:
        return ResolvedEntity(
            kind="unknown", pattern="", display_name="",
            detectors=[], is_subjective=False,
        )

    lowered = pattern.strip().lower()
    normalized = lowered.replace(" ", "_")

    # 1. Special views
    if lowered in ("concerns",):
        return ResolvedEntity(
            kind="special_view", pattern=pattern,
            display_name=pattern, detectors=[], is_subjective=False,
        )
    if lowered in ("subjective", "subjective_review"):
        return ResolvedEntity(
            kind="special_view", pattern=pattern,
            display_name=pattern, detectors=[], is_subjective=True,
        )

    # 2. Dimension name/key (from DIMENSIONS -- the mechanical ones)
    dim_lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    detectors = dim_lookup.get(normalized) or dim_lookup.get(lowered)
    if detectors:
        # Find the canonical Dimension object
        for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
            dim_norm = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
            if dim_norm == normalized or dim.name.lower() == lowered:
                return ResolvedEntity(
                    kind="dimension", pattern=pattern,
                    display_name=dim.name, detectors=list(dim.detectors),
                    is_subjective=False,  # Mechanical dimensions
                )

    # 3. Subjective dimension name/key (DISPLAY_NAMES or dimension_scores)
    display_name = scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(normalized)
    if display_name:
        return ResolvedEntity(
            kind="dimension", pattern=pattern,
            display_name=display_name, detectors=[],
            is_subjective=True,
        )
    # Check dimension_scores keys (case-insensitive)
    for ds_key, ds_val in (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).items():
        if ds_key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == normalized:
            is_subj = "subjective_assessment" in (ds_val.get("detectors") or {})
            if is_subj:
                return ResolvedEntity(
                    kind="dimension", pattern=pattern,
                    display_name=ds_key, detectors=[],
                    is_subjective=True,
                )
            # It's a mechanical dimension matched by score key
            # (e.g., "File health" matched via dimension_scores)
            return ResolvedEntity(
                kind="dimension", pattern=pattern,
                display_name=ds_key, detectors=_detectors_for_dimension_score_key(ds_key),
                is_subjective=False,
            )

    # 4. Detector name
    from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    if lowered in DETECTORS or normalized in DETECTORS:
        det_name = lowered if lowered in DETECTORS else normalized
        return ResolvedEntity(
            kind="detector", pattern=pattern,
            display_name=det_name, detectors=[det_name],
            is_subjective=False,
        )

    # 5. File path, glob, finding ID -- let scope_matches handle it
    return ResolvedEntity(
        kind="file_or_pattern", pattern=pattern,
        display_name=pattern, detectors=[], is_subjective=False,
    )


def _detectors_for_dimension_score_key(ds_key: str) -> list[str]:
    """Map a dimension_scores display key back to its detector list."""
    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
        if dim.name.lower() == ds_key.lower():
            return list(dim.detectors)
    return []
```

### 5. Restructured `cmd_show` Flow

Replace the current 100-line fallback block (lines 99-213) with a clean three-phase flow.

**Phase 1: Entity Resolution (new)**
```python
# Right after resolve_show_scope, before load_matches:
entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
```

**Phase 2: Load findings based on entity type**
```python
if entity.kind == "special_view" and not entity.is_subjective:
    # "concerns"
    _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
    return

if entity.kind == "dimension" and not entity.is_subjective:
    # Mechanical dimension: load all detectors
    matches = _load_dimension_matches(state, entity, status_filter)
elif entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
    # Subjective dimension: show score + subjective explanation
    _show_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, narrative)
    return
elif entity.kind in ("detector", "file_or_pattern", "unknown"):
    # Original scope_matches path
    matches = load_matches(state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic)
```

Where `_load_dimension_matches` calls `load_matches_for_dimension` directly (no fallback needed):

```python
def _load_dimension_matches(state, entity, status_filter):
    all_matches = []
    for detector in entity.detectors:
        matches = load_matches(state, scope=detector, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=False)
        all_matches.extend(matches)
    # Deduplicate
    seen = set()
    unique = []
    for item in all_matches:
        fid = item.get("id", "")
        if fid not in seen:
            seen.add(fid)
            unique.append(item)
    return unique
```

**Phase 3: Handle empty results for mechanical dimensions**

When a mechanical dimension has zero findings, show the score and a "clean" message:

```python
if entity.kind == "dimension" and not entity.is_subjective and not matches:
    _show_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
    return
```

```python
def _show_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity):
    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores") or {}
    dim_data = dim_scores.get(entity.display_name, {})
    score = dim_data.get("score")
    strict = dim_data.get("strict", score)
    if score is not None:
        print(colorize(
            f"  {entity.display_name}: {score:.1f}% health (strict: {strict:.1f}%)",
            "bold",
        ))
    print(colorize(
        f"  No open findings for {entity.display_name}. "
        f"Detectors: {', '.join(entity.detectors)}",
        "green",
    ))
```

This completely eliminates the misclassification -- a mechanical dimension can never reach the subjective path because entity resolution classifies it up front.

### 6. Handling the Detector-Dimension Name Collision

The resolution priority in `resolve_entity` handles this cleanly:

- `show security` --> resolves to `ResolvedEntity(kind="dimension", display_name="Security", detectors=["security", "cycles"], is_subjective=False)`
- This loads findings for both the `security` and `cycles` detectors
- If the user specifically wants ONLY the `security` detector, they can use a glob: `show security::*`

This is a **behavior change** for `show security` -- it will now show `cycles` findings too. This is intentionally correct. The old behavior (only showing `security`-detector findings) was silently incomplete.

### 7. "show concerns" Handling

The `"concerns"` special view is currently handled before `resolve_show_scope` (line 80). Move it into the entity resolution to keep all pattern dispatch in one place. The existing early-return at line 80 becomes:

```python
entity = resolve_entity(pattern_raw, state) if pattern_raw else None
if entity and entity.kind == "special_view" and pattern_raw.strip().lower() == "concerns":
    _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
    return
```

Actually, to minimize changes, the "concerns" early-return at line 80 can stay where it is. It runs before `resolve_show_scope` and has no bug. Only the post-`load_matches` fallback block needs restructuring.

#### File 2: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/cmd.py`

```python
def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    # ... existing setup (lines 62-98) stays the same ...

    # NEW: Entity resolution
    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)

    # Branch by entity kind
    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
        _show_subjective_dimension_view(state, config, entity, status_filter, narrative)
        return

    if entity.kind == "dimension" and not entity.is_subjective:
        matches = _load_matches_for_mechanical_dimension(
            state, entity, status_filter=status_filter
        )
    elif entity.kind in ("special_view",) and entity.is_subjective:
        # "subjective" / "subjective_review" views
        matches = load_matches(state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic)
    else:
        matches = load_matches(state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic)

    if not matches and entity.kind == "dimension" and not entity.is_subjective:
        _show_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
        return

    if not matches:
        # Fallback for unknown patterns -- simplified, no subjective reinterpretation
        # for mechanical dimensions (that's handled above)
        _show_no_matches(state, config, entity, pattern, pattern_raw, status_filter, narrative)
        return

    # ... rest of existing flow (lines 215-276) stays the same ...
```

**Add private helpers** in cmd.py:
- `_load_matches_for_mechanical_dimension(state, entity, status_filter)` -- loops entity.detectors, loads matches, deduplicates
- `_show_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)` -- prints score + "no findings" in green
- `_show_subjective_dimension_view(state, config, entity, status_filter, narrative)` -- extracts the existing subjective display logic from lines 128-165
- `_show_no_matches(state, config, entity, pattern, pattern_raw, status_filter, narrative)` -- simplified version of the current else-block at lines 167-213, handling the "subjective" special view and the generic "No findings" hint


#### File 3: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/render.py`

### 9. What "Mechanical Dimension with No Findings" Should Render

Current (buggy):
```
  Security: 100.0% health (strict: 100.0%)
  'security' is a subjective dimension -- its score comes from design reviews, not code findings.
```

Proposed (correct):
```
  Security: 100.0% health (strict: 100.0%)
  No open findings for Security. Detectors: security, cycles.
```

This is accurate, helpful, and clearly distinguishes mechanical dimensions from subjective ones.

### 10. Test Plan

Add tests to `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/test_cmd_show.py`:

2. **`test_show_security_includes_cycles_findings`**: `show security` with open `cycles` findings should include them in the output.

4. **`test_resolve_entity_dimension_over_detector`**: `resolve_entity("security", state)` should return `kind="dimension"`, not `kind="detector"`.

5. **`test_resolve_entity_file_health`**: `resolve_entity("file_health", state)` should return `kind="dimension"` with `detectors=["structural"]`.

6. **`test_resolve_entity_unknown_pattern`**: `resolve_entity("src/foo.py", state)` should return `kind="file_or_pattern"`.

7. **`test_is_dimension_score_subjective`**: Verify the centralized check works for both mechanical and subjective dimension data dicts.

Existing test `test_show_subjective_dimension_by_key` should continue passing -- it tests `show naming_quality` which is a subjective dimension and should still show the subjective message.

### 11. Migration Path for Inline Subjective Checks (Follow-up)

The 6 inline `"subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})` checks can be replaced with the centralized function in a separate PR:

| File | Line | Current |
|------|------|---------|
| `intelligence/narrative/dimensions.py` | 48 | inline check |
| `engine/_scoring/results/core.py` | 184 | inline check |
| `engine/_state/scoring.py` | 371 | inline check |
| `app/output/scorecard_parts/dimensions.py` | 50, 146 | inline checks |
| `app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_llm.py` | 87 | inline check |

To do this, `is_dimension_score_subjective` would need to live in the scoring policy layer rather than `show/scope.py`. The recommended final home is `desloppify/engine/_scoring/policy/core.py`, re-exported through `desloppify/scoring.py`. But this is out of scope for the current fix.

### 12. Risks and Mitigations

**Risk**: `show security` behavior change (now includes `cycles` findings).
**Mitigation**: This is strictly more correct. No user expects `show security` to hide `cycles` findings that belong to the Security dimension.

**Risk**: `resolve_entity` adds import-time cost.
**Mitigation**: It only builds the dimension lookup (already done by `_build_dimension_lookup`). The cost is negligible.

**Risk**: New code path for mechanical dimensions not exercised.
**Mitigation**: Add the 7 tests described above.
